# Retrospective — v1-9-1

## Goal

# v1.9.1 devline objective (condensed)  Full approved objective: \`docs/superpowers/objectives/2026-10-17-v1.9.1-objective.md\` (sha256 \`1ad30976e2da578221351121f0177bc77533b667ff8172818db01671f8a841c7\`, commit \`ae85aace\`, approved by the user 2026-10-17 together with Claude's pending design decisions). The full file is authoritative; this condensed copy exists because the devline Context Pack accepts at most 16 KiB of objective text.  Baseline: tag \`v1.9.0\` = \`402d2d3e\`. Worktree \`.worktrees/workbench-v1.9.1\`, branch \`workbench-v1.9.1\`, line \`v1-9-1\`.  ## Goal  Make the Arboris Agent a "Claude Code for research": the user chooses **Ask every step** or **Auto within plan**, both polished for daily use; given raw data and a research question the Agent organizes and completes a full, review-ready analysis (data understanding, estimation, diagnostics, multi-specification robustness, tables, report); the plan is always visible and editable; the user can steer, interrupt and undo a whole run at any time; runs continue in the background. Improvement is proven by public benchmarks, replication of published papers and many-analysts data, not by self-assessment.  ## Principles  1. Both modes are first class. After a confirmation the Agent continues on its own; the user never has to say "continue". 2. Safety boundaries do not relax with the mode: raw-data changes, exports, egress and registered high-risk operations need an    explicit approval each time in both modes; interrupt is always available. 3. A lineage branch is the checkpoint; conversation and lineage rewind together. 4. Plan approval writes \`criteria_fixed\`; planned analyses are pre-specified, steps added after seeing results are exploratory. 5. Self-check against the playbook's completion criteria before declaring done. 6. Evaluation-driven: measure the v1.9.0 baseline and pre-register thresholds before implementing. DeepSeek V4.1 Flash    (\`deepseek-flash\`) only; no spending cap but no waste (record/replay for regression; real calls for baseline, two mid-term    rounds, final and browser acceptance). 7. Refactoring serves this version's capabilities; behaviour-preserving; judged by touch points per new capability and dependency    structure, not line counts.  ## Milestones  - **M0 baseline and pre-registration**: failure taxonomy from real session JSONL; full request-body and cache-hit profile; dev set   ≥ 60 end-to-end tasks (both modes) and held-out set ≥ 20 with expert rubrics calibrated by human scoring; BLADE, QRData,   DiscoveryBench subsets; red-card (Silberzahn et al. 2018) and Huntington-Klein et al. 2021 many-analysts data; replication   challenge on 5 AEA/openICPSR packages; baseline on v1.9.0; thresholds written into the full objective and approved by the user. - **M1 foundation**: behaviour-preserving splits of \`planning_agent\`, \`workflow_contracts\`, \`workflow_runtime\`, the touched parts of   \`orchestrator\` and \`NotebookService\`, and \`http/agent_routes.py\`; one unified agent loop and one identity (Notebook planning becomes   a tool or skill; execution is a tool call whose result returns to the model; plan-based budgets); one typed operation-request   contract replacing the four proposal paths; durable, resumable, background turns with run-level cancellation; headless   \`workbench agent run\` with an event stream; record/replay. - **M2 modes, plan, steering**: the two execution modes and permission rules (session "don't ask again", project allow/ask/deny rules   that can never loosen the safety floor) as an exhaustively tested state machine; plan object (DAG, versions, diffs, preflight,   time estimates, user editing) and plan panel; ledger events \`criteria_fixed\`, \`question_change\`, \`specification_status\`; steer,   follow-up, interrupt, undo APIs and UI; slash commands; notifications; blind plan comparison replaces the Plans view; retire the   Notebook page and the classic shell. - **M3 analysis capability**: full-analysis playbooks with completion self-checks and bounded failure recovery; multi-specification   robustness with specification curves; an isolated reviewer sub-agent; tool ergonomics rework driven by the failure taxonomy,   parallel read-only tools, cache-friendly stable prefixes. - **M4 extensions, safety, observability, performance**: skills (\`SKILL.md\`, built-in / personal / project, progressive loading,   scripts only through the capability-factory sandbox), external tools (MCP-like, under egress control) and hooks, each with   admission, provenance, version and disable switch; built-in skills absorbing \`nature-statistics\` and \`nature-figure\` under   Apache-2.0 attribution; prompt-injection red team (≥ 30 cases); per-step timing, tokens, cache hits and cost; vectorized sample   fingerprints byte-identical to the current hashes. - **M5 final evaluation and release preparation**: dev and held-out sets once each, external subsets, many-analysts, replication   challenge, ablations; three real-browser scenarios; docs and handoff.  ## Acceptance  Safety floor (independent of baseline): prompt-injection success 0; unauthorized state change or high-risk execution 0; numeric grounding violations 0; "I am read-only" after a confirmation 0; times the user must say "continue" 0.  Initial targets (may only be raised after the baseline; lowering needs a devline GAP event and user approval): dev completion ≥ 90 % per mode; rubric mean ≥ 4.0/5; pass^3 ≥ 75 %; held-out completion ≥ 80 % (run once); tool rejection ≤ 3 % (8.6 % in real sessions); argument-format retries median 0 and ≤ 2 per task; Auto-mode user actions = 1 plan approval plus high-risk confirmations; replication ≥ 4 of 5 main tables to printed precision; red-card main estimate inside the central 90 % of team estimates with every decision justified; BLADE/QRData not below the strongest comparable published baseline; prompt cache hit ≥ 70 % on multi-step tasks; first plan ≤ 15 s; non-model overhead per step median ≤ 300 ms; interrupt ≤ 1 s, long-computation cancel ≤ 2 s, plan panel update ≤ 200 ms; 1M-row ingest + OLS ≤ 8 s with all 14 \`perf_1m\` budgets met.  Browser scenarios with real \`deepseek-flash\` at 1512 × 982 in light and dark: Ask every step on the IV example (edit one form, skip one step, one "don't ask again"); Auto within plan on a panel DID project (edit plan, parallel run, steer, interrupt, close the window and reopen on the notification, undo and rerun); open question with blind comparison, Run anyway, specification curve and correct pre-specified/exploratory report sections. Host full gate, two self-reviews, one Codex evaluation lane.  ## Out of scope  Inference kernel, HC/CR defaults, wild bootstrap and Rust components (v1.9.2); other model providers; journal writing packs, journal figure export and publication tables; splitting \`data_operations.py\`, \`api.ts\` and context sub-modules; quant direction; windows under 1200 px.  ## Hard constraints  Fully usable without a model; no feature loss when retiring the Notebook page and classic shell; structural guards from the kickoff handoff §4; never \`pip install -e\` or \`npm install\` in the worktree, new dependencies need user approval; external datasets and replication packages only under \`local/\` with source, version, licence and checksum recorded; merge, tag, push and Release need user authorization.

Fixed-input comparison showed slower median time and RSS beyond the original resource cap; timing noise also failed.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Measure the actual owner workload and requested allocations before promoting vectorized metadata code; reject regressions without replacing or excluding failed evidence.
- #194 2026-10-29T13:49:57.357Z `display_reference_confused_with_registered_evidence`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Recorded development analysis copied a display result alias into a registered artifact slot, so the strict completion gate stayed partial while prose claimed question completion. Repeated string-shaped changes and hidden head/inspection errors prevented bounded repair. Unknown inspection errors could previously return an unchecked request.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Separate display identifiers from canonical registered references, reject invalid concrete evidence without blocking legitimate partial plans, and preserve actionable bounded errors and truthful qualified completion.
- #198 2026-10-29T16:27:59.473Z `serial_queue_exhausted_command_budget`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The fresh two-task cohort used one serialized HTTP lease. This directly localizes the cancellation and does not prove an upstream provider failure.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Match task concurrency to the serialized provider throughput and unchanged per-command wall. Preserve the timeout, failed tasks and unknown usage; do not bypass the stop or blame the provider without evidence. Prepare a serial-task candidate for an explicit continuation decision.
- #205 2026-10-29T19:09:20.941Z `production_moments_memory_and_process_noise_gate`; cause_status: `suspected`; cause: The production moments public API preserves original-input exact certificates and is 14.4 times faster in the fixed component lane, but RSS median grows 8.4 percent and RSS/process noise exceed five percent. Retain failed A/B data and source binaries; a revised observer must re-register and preserve all numerical/resource/noise gates rather than transferring local speed into default-path acceptance.
- #215 2026-10-29T21:48:16.447Z `bounded_eval_capacity_stop_and_tool_rejections`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The frozen75dd continuation ended on a complete error-onlyNVIDIA429after the explicitly approved30shared recoveries. These local failures are separate from transport refusal.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Preserve unknown usage and both local/transport failures; do not claim a rate-ceiling violation, silently raise recovery limits, or credit new product fixes to an old frozen cohort.
- #219 2026-10-30T01:29:11.568Z `owned_async_child_completed_parent_receipt_not_reconciled`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The common Auto/headless proposal runner returned after a single async rerun submit. Every UI/Auto/headless owner must use the same original provenance reconciler before subsequent result reads; keep the original approval, source, budget and idempotency key. Cancellation preserves takeover and missing original WAL remains explicit recovery.
- #225 2026-10-30T11:59:09.978Z `native_companion_recovery_hidden`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Any same-run diagnostic suppressed the exact native model companion; AR recovery was absent from default operation digests and generic criteria guidance overwrote specific next steps.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Native diagnostic binding must use the exact model companion. - #231 2026-10-31T05:17:00.720Z `http200_no_body_local_idle_cancel`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The route returned HTTP200 SSE headers but no captured body. Upstream lack-of-body cause remains unproven.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Preserve received headers, generated-content evidence and the local cancellation actor separately. The causal ordering and ownership cleanup remain unproven.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Keep task outcome, actual provider lifetime, interrupted usage and lease state separate. - #267 2026-11-04T00:31:06.000Z `iv_refused_missing_instrument_rows`; cause_status: `known`; cause: linearmodels' formula interface raises on a missing outcome, endogenous or instrument value (it drops a missing exogenous one) and the product never dropped incomplete rows before an IV fit, so every IV run on such data stopped at estimation.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A reference library's missing-value policy can differ by role; check every role, not only the regressors.

- #327 2026-11-05T19:50:16.529Z `native_research_component_noise`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The native local-level per-call warm duration varied above the five-percent ceiling in the first matched comparison.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Use predeclared same-input blocks to measure millisecond component calls and retain the original observations. - #382 2026-11-07T10:35:07.736Z `agent_published_denied_workflow`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The Agent published a pending workflow before checking the project deny rules, and recovery did not distinguish a matching refit from a forbidden new model specification.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Apply the shared deny decision before publishing a card. - #384 2026-11-08T00:18:56.000Z `agent_answers_binary_outcome_from_seed_ols`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The session focus told the Agent the selected import node should be acted on, and nothing in the OLS result said that OLS does not serve a binary outcome, so the Agent read the seed OLS as the requested analysis and answered with a linear probability model.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Probe the recorded request counterfactually before changing prompts; one-step probes did not move the model, so the fix states product facts (the focus names a selection, not the analysis; a declared outcome.type_served caution names the families that serve the outcome) instead of forcing Logit.
- #389 2026-11-08T05:33:16.000Z `outcome_family_label_keyerror_in_retest`; cause_status: `known`; cause: At aeb81ade the method check withdrew the imported-OLS draft and the Agent went straight to model.rerun as Logit, computed marginal effects and a Probit comparison, but the turn ended on KeyError 'outcome_family': the partial-note labels were a literal per-playbook table. The gate's ready report.compose also named checks_logit_1, which report sources refused, costing one failed report.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A new playbook value must be exercised through every consumer of the playbook name, the person-facing partial note and the report included; offline tests stopped at evaluate and missed both. - #390 2026-11-08T12:08:56.000Z `outcome_family_sensitivity_unreachable_with_real_fits`; cause_status: `known`; cause: In the 501cdda6 round binary_logit.open.r1 fitted Logit by model.rerun and a Probit comparison, but the gate kept outcome_family:sensitivity:distinct_point_estimation missing: logit and probit model_result payloads carry no point_estimation_fingerprint, so no comparison could ever be distinct. The repair step pointed at prepared options that were model.genesis (refused there); the Agent never composed a report and repeated one partial answer through 15 recovery prompts until the nudge budget. open.r2 was stopping the same way and the user stopped the round.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Gate tests for a playbook must use real fits from the engine, not hand-built payloads: the outcome_family unit fixture supplied a fingerprint no real logit or probit fit writes. The new tests run real Logit/Probit workflows, and the recorded open.r1 project now misses only its report.
- #391 2026-11-08T17:21:28.000Z `gate_compose_report_refused_by_report_sources`; cause_status: `known`; cause: At b25d3a1b binary_logit.open.r1 met the outcome_family model, diagnostics and sensitivity requirements, then looped on report:registered_nonempty: the gate binds computed results (statistical_exploration) and requires the report to cover them, but report_sources accepted only diagnostic artifact types, so the gate's own compose_report failed and narrower reports did not count. The 128 evidence rows were also first-come, which would have left the marginal effects out of the report.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Two consumers of one evidence set must share one definition: report sources now use the gate's computed_result predicate, and the regression test runs the gate's own compose_report through report.compose on real fits and a real exploration until the gate reports nothing missing.
- #392 2026-11-08T18:47:19.000Z `named_diagnostic_displaced_fit_check_record`; cause_status: `known`; cause: At 428ee909 binary_logit.open.r1 bound Logit primary, Probit sensitivity, margins and a report, then looped on outcome_family:diagnostics:computed_and_model_bound: the Agent named diagnostics_logit_1, and derive_evidence skipped the primary's own checks_logit_1 whenever any same-run diagnostic was named. The evidence catalog did not list check records (diagnostics_meet_requirements false) and evaluate emitted no repair step, so each recovery nudge was unactionable.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A requirement must be reachable from what the gate shows: the record a check reads is bound by the Workbench regardless of what else the Agent names, listed in the catalog, and named in a repair step whenever it is missing. A missing item with empty repair_steps is a gate defect.
- #394 2026-11-08T21:35:17.000Z `denied_genesis_recovery_did_not_reach_refit`; cause_status: `known`; cause: At 7821c4c7 binary_logit.open.r3 proposed model.genesis (Logit with age and urban controls), which the project denies. The recovery told it to refit only a model whose predictors match the intended specification, without naming the model node; one contract inspection on stage:cleaned failed, and the Agent ran only proportion, chi-square and Mann-Whitney tests. The gate's no_eligible_primary repair listed no candidates and did not name the imported OLS that model.rerun could refit with Logit. It stopped on nudge_budget after 40 requests.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A denial's recovery and the gate's repair must name the permitted next operation and its exact target: the existing model node and the estimator change, with the blocked extra predictors stated as a limitation.
- #408 2026-11-10T00:30:00.000Z `perf_footprint_outliers_from_spotlight_compression`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The intermittent +400-1100 MiB footprint runs (in both versions; mostly the first run after another heavy workload) are memory compression, not program memory. Sampling task_vm_info and malloc_zone_statistics every 5-10 ms in 1M-row IV reference runs right after a 1M-row OLS workflow: outliers (2807, 2821 MiB) had 427/391 MiB compressed and 777/704 MiB reusable at the peak, normal runs (2085-2388) 0-26 compressed and 1294-1600 reusable, with malloc bytes in use equal in all (2146-2176 MiB). With every run directory under a Spotlight-excluded .noindex folder, the same sequence gave 2060-2319 MiB, compressed 0-17, no outlier (9 runs each). Host: 24 GiB, 3.5 GiB free, 5.8 GiB compressor, mds_stores 1.3 GiB; each measured run writes about 200 MB that Spotlight then indexes.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Before attributing peak-memory variance to code, split footprint into live malloc bytes, reusable and compressed pages; self-written output that Spotlight indexes can compress the next run's pages.
- #425 2026-11-14T05:00:00.000Z `full_suite_restored_after_parallel_commits`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The four full-suite failures found in the review of fa70e117 are fixed: the statistics-stage bit-identity harness takes the new native_math._pair_ranks signature and the guard runs and passes again; the capability pin counts prediction.native_forest_apply (181 capabilities, 0 gaps); the forest-apply description fits the 90-character limit. Full backend suite: 14,061 passed, 0 failed. The three design concerns of that review (forced recovery on focused questions, no inline step contracts, request admission margin) are unchanged and wait for the user's decision.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A changed private signature needs a search of the tests that patch it; run the full suite before calling a batch green.
- #467 2026-11-20T10:41:58.829Z `v191_notebook_mixed_model_artifacts`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The planner computed required artifacts from every model step. A top-level label must not grant unrelated artifact requirements.

## All errors

- #2 2026-10-18T03:51:04.000Z `eval_recorder_double_decode`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The evaluation recorder passed the provider response on with its content-encoding header after reading the decoded body, so the client decoded it a second time, failed, and the adapter resent requests that had succeeded (three identical requests per step in the pilot).; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: An evaluation instrument is code under test: verify it on a pilot (request counts, hashes) before trusting a round.
- #18 2026-10-18T18:28:58.009Z `core_dispatch_command_identity_collision`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Initial invocation metadata used a new Core command even when the server dispatcher already owned a command; an existing dispatch proposal test exposed the mismatch.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Reuse the authority owner identity; model parameters and trusted server context are different trust boundaries.
- #24 2026-10-18T18:53:29.900Z `forecast_metric_complement_misdiagnosis`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The investigator initially treated interval coverage and separately configured lower-quantile exceptions as complementary events.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Trace each statistic to its event definition before imposing a cross-field identity. A number-bag check does not certify field semantics.
- #29 2026-10-19T12:41:49.968Z `hash_seed_changed_published_workflow_enum`; cause_status: `known`; cause: A real third-step killed-process recovery refused the tool contract because a set-derived operation enum changed order across Python hash seeds.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Make model-facing declarations deterministic at their owner; do not weaken full-field replay or contract comparison to hide ordering drift.
- #114 2026-10-22T07:53:52.000Z `tape_timestamp_misread`; cause_status: `known`; cause: I treated the tape's 'at' field as the response end time; recorder.py writes the request start time.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Read the writer of a recorded field before computing from it, and measure a proposed saving on recorded data before quoting it.
- #134 2026-10-22T20:21:05.000Z `partial_notice_stacked_and_wrong_language`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Continuations set the command text to None, so language detection fell back to English, and publication prepended a notice to answers that already began with one.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A continuation inherits the person's last request; published notices replace, not stack.
- #138 2026-10-23T10:39:25.000Z `harness_buffering_misread_as_provider_stalls`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The evaluation recorder read each streamed reply whole before passing it on, so the product's 90 s no-progress bound measured total generation time; answers longer than 90 s were cut. I attributed the resulting Hy3 and Step-5 'stalls' to the WorkBuddy route and changed the guard and retry policy around them.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: When a failure has a sharp ceiling equal to one of our own bounds, check the harness path end to end (probe without it) before blaming the provider.
- #195 2026-10-29T13:54:21.628Z `evaluation_evidence_reference_corrected`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Failure event60a73f72-e5ac-4a73-bc87-bd2c53c0bacf accidentally used the mutable progress checklist as its evidence reference. This correction points to the full tracked evaluation report; the original failure and every raw outcome remain unchanged.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Use the complete tracked evaluation artifact rather than a mutable one-page checklist as the primary failure evidence.
- #199 2026-10-29T16:32:56.088Z `benchmark_cli_failed_to_signal_gate_failure`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The offline pipeline driver wrote a failed noise gate report but returned None from main, so its actual process exit was zero. The original failed report and measurements were retained and were never declared full acceptance.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A measurement CLI must propagate failed gates as a nonzero process status. Preserve the exact measured driver and raw report when repairing reporting semantics after measurement.
- #212 2026-10-29T21:10:44.730Z `derived_component_ratio_correction`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The preceding gate event transcribed an overprecise derived ratio incorrectly. Recompute directly from the unchanged report medians; raw observations,14.3x rounded report, numerical equality and every gate are unchanged.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Calculate derived report values from their registered machine evidence, never transcribe additional precision by hand.
- #220 2026-10-30T07:34:40.566Z `benchmark_registration_invocation_metadata_changed`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The component runner pins the complete environment. Separate terminal invocations changed CODEX_TOOL_CALL_ID and the original launch refused before any product worker; source and numerical settings were unchanged.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Register and execute a fixed-environment measurement under one actual parent invocation. Preserve the complete inherited environment and original failed registration; do not drop varying fields or relax source, thread, noise or resource checks. A repaired preflight does not establish performance acceptance.
- #227 2026-10-30T12:51:36.699Z `supervisor_enospc_stop_persistence_failed`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Local ENOSPC prevented supervisor heartbeat and emergency stop lock creation; supervisor exited120 while its child remained alive. Root later persisted stop and drained the existing request.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: A persistent request guard cannot replace OS child ownership when disk writes fail. Preserve the original IO failure and stop owned children if emergency stop persistence itself fails.
- #234 2026-10-31T19:01:20.000Z `machine_sleep_read_as_provider_failure`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The lease and tape used the wall clock while the turn budget used the monotonic clock, and evaluation rounds did not hold the machine awake.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Compare wall and monotonic time and check the power log before attributing a long request to the provider.
- #237 2026-11-01T01:41:00.000Z `eval_request_cap_exceeded_on_ledger_swap`; cause_status: `known`; cause: run4.py gave a stopped lane a fresh ledger at the full per-lane limit without deducting requests already used.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Any ledger replacement must carry the requests already used, since a cap counted per ledger is not a cap.
- #239 2026-11-01T09:23:19.000Z `eval_tasks_interrupted_by_lid_sleep`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The laptop lid closed during the round; caffeinate does not stop clamshell sleep.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Before a long round, check the machine stays open; decide what happens to interrupted tasks before reading their grades.
- #240 2026-11-01T09:23:19.000Z `tool_import_dropped_workflow_step`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Importing the report service while the tool registry loaded built the workflow step registry before imputation methods registered.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Registries built at import depend on import order: new tools import heavy services when they run, and a subprocess test guards it.
- #315 2026-11-05T18:39:01.899Z `default_gate_optional_evidence`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The v6 default gate incorrectly required the optional advanced statistics artifact for registered DID, panel, and Cox workloads. The worker also classified a statistics capture failure as an inference-kernel failure.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Require all seven core packets, require advanced evidence for predeclared workloads and whenever registered, and compare all published fields including presence. - #324 2026-11-05T19:37:28.478Z `native_library_in_place_publication`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Copying a rebuilt Rust dylib over a previously mapped inode caused macOS to terminate a fresh loader process. The signed Cargo target with the same contents loaded successfully.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Publish native libraries with a fresh inode and atomic replacement. Verify bytes, source and ABI against the build receipt after publication.
- #413 2026-11-10T23:00:00.000Z `agent_tool_call_errors_reduced`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Tool-call errors in the 5 NVIDIA binary_logit positions (~29): fixed the general ones without loosening approvals or validation: 152c8319 update_plan without steps keeps the current steps (1); a7a10168 a completion source named by its dataset node binds that node's registered dataset, without reading data (2); f9cd1245 the refit outcome names the conversation head and the exact model_ref/model_run_id fields (1 ACTIVE_HEAD_MISMATCH proposal + 3 reads, 2 more wrong-field proposals). Not fixed: broken JSON in changes (4, model output), depends_on on earlier executed steps (3; validator has no session state and report.compose required_steps would still fail), AME/alias refusals (correct). Replay finding: agent_eval_run wrote the M5 deny rule only for held-out/formal runs, so dev-round replays diverged at the first denial (looked like a denied genesis becoming a proposal; the product still denies it); e83e9c7b adds --project-deny-policy. With it, goal/open.r1/open.r2 replay unchanged, specific.r1 request 3 now succeeds, completion recoveries replace nudges (open.r1 2, open.r2 1, open.r3 4). Earlier dev replays without the rule were not evidence past the denial. - #450 2026-11-19T08:24:11.624Z `nvidia_named_tool_schema_rejected`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The official NVIDIA Ultra endpoint rejects the named tool grammar when the plan JSON Schema includes not, although the same tool declaration works in automatic mode.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Select automatic tool mode for NVIDIA Ultra and keep the one-tool surface and Workbench validation. - #452 2026-11-19T09:11:36.030Z `model_rerun_public_schema_full_gate`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Two route assertions still required server-owned pins in the model-facing rerun schema.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Test model-facing required inputs and backward compatibility at the route, then separately verify full persisted binding and stale-head refusal.

## All gaps

- #6 2026-10-18T12:42:18.000Z `ols_cluster_column_deferred_to_v192`; cause_status: `known`; cause: OLS clustered standard errors need a cluster column, but model.genesis forbids entity_col for OLS and OLS model_options have no cluster field, so the Agent cannot cluster an OLS fit by classroom (baseline cluster_rct.specific, the STAR replication). The fix belongs in contracts/model, a protected path of this line reserved for the v1.9.2 inference kernel.; resolution: `open`; lesson: A capability the Agent cannot name in a workflow does not exist for it, even when the UI form can do it.

- #11 2026-10-18T17:25:33.962Z `extension_cost_unchanged`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Mechanical extraction improves ownership but does not remove the two production files and four implementation locations required by the fixed generic statistical-family extension probe. - #15 2026-10-18T17:59:13.481Z `m4_end_to_end_8s_unmet`; cause_status: `unknown`; cause: Candidate perf_1m printed ingest plus OLS at 29.05 seconds, above the approved 8-second objective despite the decimal sample-fingerprint microbenchmark improvement. Remaining end-to-end cost needs profiling.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Keep the 8-second target unchanged. - #39 2026-10-19T20:31:07.435Z `workflow_extension_touchpoints`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Pure extraction keeps the existing separate declaration, semantic validation, column-resolution and execution branches.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Report source decomposition and extension cost separately; shortening entries does not reduce operation-registration seams.
- #102 2026-10-21T14:52:17.919Z `objective_performance_and_real_acceptance_open`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The prior resource-regression acceptance did not certify the objective OLS 8-second target or all analysis/skill/browser evaluations. - #103 2026-10-21T16:29:22.214Z `small_candidate_round_incomplete`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Paid development replay exposed ambiguous primary evidence, wrong report refs, missing Agent AR reachability and repeated partial completion. Return typed, source-verified artifact bindings and distinguish primary ambiguity from a missing model without weakening scientific completion.
- #120 2026-10-22T12:15:11.000Z `reviewer_subpath_refs_rejected`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The review parser accepts an evidence_ref only when it equals a packet key; the reviewer cites paths inside keys (plan.criteria[2], diagnostic:0.summary...).; resolution: `open`; lesson: Validate model citations by what they point to, not by exact spelling, and keep refusing references outside the supplied evidence.
- #122 2026-10-22T12:24:02.000Z `reviewer_subpath_refs_rejected`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The review parser accepts an evidence_ref only when it equals a packet key; the reviewer cites paths inside keys (plan.criteria[2], diagnostic:0.summary...).; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Validate model citations by what they point to, not by exact spelling, and keep refusing references outside the supplied evidence.
- #129 2026-10-22T16:35:05.000Z `grader_v2_sample_truth`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The leverage scenario scored estimates against the population slope 1.0, but the goal variant's fixed draw supports 1.276 on its clean rows, so no correct analysis could pass.; resolution: `accepted`; lesson: Score a simulated task against what its fixed draw supports, and check every fixed seed's oracle before freezing a grader.
- #133 2026-10-22T20:21:05.000Z `missing_sensitivity_had_no_next_action`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The completion gate named a missing distinct-point-estimation or influence-sample comparison but gave no executable next step, and its next_step asked for the report first.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Every requirement the gate reports as missing needs an executable next step derived from server-known facts; ordering matters when a later product must cover an earlier one.
- #141 2026-10-25T08:26:02.000Z `influence_option_without_identifier`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The completion gate required a high-influence sample comparison but offered a refit only when a unique identifying column existed; without one no option was offered and the model could not construct it.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A gate requirement must be satisfiable for every dataset shape it can fire on; when the server cannot build the step, say why instead of repeating the requirement.
- #154 2026-10-28T05:22:47.927Z `native_kendall_component_noise_uncertified`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Baseline-only whole-worker RSS variation was 5.273364 percent in five calibrations, exceeding the unchanged less-than-five-percent requirement. The observed paired medians therefore do not certify native acceleration.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Keep calibration failures and raw observations even when candidate medians look better; component measurements do not certify default-path end-to-end performance.
- #188 2026-10-29T08:25:55.973Z `native_component_vs_default_acceptance`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Native AR and large canonical encoding show exact component benefits; corrected Agent replay has stable local gains but whole-worker and process noise exceed five percent, associated with imports. Default workflow and integer-rank coverage still pending.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Do not promote component speedup or a stable local phase into full-workflow acceptance. Keep every failed calibration and protocol deviation, attribute phases before adjusting the experiment, and retain the eight-second OLS gate.
- #193 2026-10-29T11:57:36.530Z `default_ols_absolute_budget_unmet_after_native`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Native rank kernels and owned two-lane scheduling improve the real default workflow, but end-to-end diagnostics, figures and identity work still exceed the 8 second objective. Component gains cannot close the absolute gate.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Keep improvements that meet numerical and relative resource gates, while retaining the absolute end-to-end failure and profiling the remaining real owner work.
- #196 2026-10-29T15:17:15.495Z `exact_moment_ar_time_noise_retained`; cause_status: `unknown`; cause: Exact-moment AR retains the original input and complete proof, but calibration and full-process time noise exceed the unchanged five-percent gate. The observed large improvement does not authorize production switching.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Preserve failed calibration, separate mathematical equality from timing acceptance, and investigate phase noise before another distinct measurement.
- #203 2026-10-29T17:49:49.639Z `caller_argument_fault_misattributed_to_platform`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The finalized frozen IV task sent nine malformed changes strings despite an object-only schema and six successful multi-step execution receipts. Twelve later public replies nevertheless blamed platform deserialization. The final required influence-sample comparison did not execute.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Localize a claimed platform failure with immutable wire arguments, schema and real receipts. State caller type errors and non-execution explicitly without coercing malformed strings or taking model self-diagnosis as evidence. Offline feedback repair does not establish live improvement.
- #216 2026-10-29T23:18:30.782Z `approved_promises_need_typed_producer_evidence`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The retained approved Panel plan passed its family playbook while six criteria and nine expected outputs had no deterministic proof. The shared delivery check now preserves that playbook pass but marks overall partial; exact registered request and product proofs remain separate from execution receipts.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Verify each explicit promise using its fixed scientific definition and original producer artifacts. Preserve partial and unsupported states; reuse old results without inventing execution history, and keep scoped checks distinct from whole-release acceptance.
- #221 2026-10-30T08:33:25.685Z `typed_iv_ar_producer_evidence_not_available_to_delivery_verifier`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The real fitted IV definition was hashed then discarded from completion products, and typed criteria did not support the existing AR producer. Valid human-confirmed products therefore remained unavailable to machine deliverable verification; generic recovery wording also conflated Ask confirmation with Auto plan approval.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Persist small actual producer definitions with the original fingerprint and verify typed scientific products against the original execution, registered source and approval basis. Existing reports or prose do not replace AR evidence; old missing definitions remain partial. Ask can reuse an exact human-confirmed request while Auto retains approved-plan requirements.
- #224 2026-10-30T10:02:13.956Z `contract_projection_and_iv_interpretation_gaps`; cause_status: `known`; cause: One complete contract result was carried in thirty-three later wire requests although exact schema fields already existed in each request. The AR public view prioritized a large proof before set meaning, first-stage structured fields were absent from its summary, and broad no-rerun/numeric-check wording overstated restrictions and verification scope.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Reuse only structurally identical declarations proven present in the same model request; preserve durable ToolResults, source and approvals and retain changed schemas in full. Expose set topology, actual named diagnostics and proof scope explicitly. Exact numerical-field checks do not certify free-text science. Existing work needs binding, while justified missing work may use the original permission path. Live accuracy and latency remain unverified.
- #229 2026-10-31T05:04:32.909Z `diagnostic_risk_omitted_from_model_view`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The persisted native diagnostic had Cook and high-influence facts, but inspection exposed only preview highlights and omitted the risk section. Delivery completion, method sufficiency and estimate recovery are separate evidence.
- #235 2026-10-31T19:01:20.000Z `completion_gate_unsatisfiable_or_lost_update`; cause_status: `known`; cause: An Agent-chosen estimand no producer can certify, receipts that covered only the first plan step, and one refused done claim discarding the primary choice.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A requirement must come from the person or the data and be satisfiable; a refused part of an update must not discard the valid rest.
- #236 2026-11-01T01:40:00.000Z `import_run_nodes_without_identity`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The import-only path recorded graph nodes but never wrote node_index.json, so node tools refused them as CONTEXT_STALE.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A new run path must pass the same addressability check as the full pipeline, and a round's refusal reasons must be read while it runs, not only its score.
- #238 2026-11-01T03:54:00.000Z `feature_demo_used_seeded_message`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The attachment display was shown with a message written directly into a session, while the real model check ran in another project the user was not looking at.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Demonstrate a feature by a real turn in the interface the user is looking at; a seeded message may only be used for layout and must be disclosed.
- #243 2026-11-01T14:40:00.000Z `memory_spike_unattributed`; cause_status: `unknown`; cause: The system low-memory dialog showed Claude and its children at 21.04 GB while the 40-task round ran; it had fallen to 3.5 GB by the first check. Live processes never peaked above 1.3 GB, so the holder exited unobserved. A replay of the one suspect run peaked at 0.35 GB. Separately macOS refuses RLIMIT_AS, so sandboxed code had no effective memory limit.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Sample memory for the whole process tree during long runs; a peak of a process that exited cannot be recovered afterwards.
- #245 2026-11-01T15:42:39.000Z `memory_spike_correction`; cause_status: `unknown`; cause: Correction to memory_spike_unattributed: its resolution value says resolved, but the holder of the 21.04 GB was never identified. The sampler ran until the round ended at about 4-second intervals, peaked at 4.23 GB, and never fired its guard, so the spike did not repeat.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Do not mark a spike resolved until its holder is identified.
- #247 2026-11-01T17:16:53.000Z `real_turn_found_display_bugs`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Display work passed unit tests but a real turn showed raw result JSON filling the panel, Esc wired only to the old composer, and no note after a stop. - #248 2026-11-01T17:27:50.000Z `question_gate_false_partial`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The focused-question gate rejected workflow capability results as computed results and never bound results the Agent did not name, so answers computed from the data were marked not tied to a result.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A gate's refusal is also a claim to the reader; replay it over recorded sessions, in both directions, before and after changing it.
- #251 2026-11-01T21:53:43.000Z `stuck_operation_fix_did_not_raise_accuracy`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The 40-task round on 938c4527 removed pending operations (12 to 0) and timeouts (7 to 2), but tasks that used to time out now answer wrongly; 30/40 both rounds, McNemar p=1.; resolution: `open`; lesson: A timeout on a wrong task can hide a second cause of the wrong answer; judge a fix by accuracy on tasks that did not produce it.
- #252 2026-11-01T23:43:18.000Z `fixes_show_no_accuracy_gain_on_fresh_tasks`; cause_status: `known`; cause: On 15 unseen QRData tasks, c0693c47 scored 8 and 79dc2aae scored 9 (McNemar p=1). - #273 2026-11-04T17:33:01.896Z `fast_iv_resource_noise_remains_unaccepted`; cause_status: `unknown`; cause: Idle-host a133fbdf paired measurements passed numerical identity, declared tolerances, repeated own bits, host/source and requested figure delivery. - #307 2026-11-05T08:44:30.370Z `ols_result_row_storage`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Legacy OLS results store sample order and a sorted copy of the same identifiers, and artifact consumers verify the original JSON bytes.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Version a separate storage representation and verify exact restoration before using it at an immutable artifact boundary.
- #308 2026-11-05T08:50:53.553Z `fast_iv_stale_mount_identity`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The IV acceptance driver copied a registered device number after it changed, and the worker rejected the stale file identity despite a matching input SHA-256.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: After validating the original content SHA and stable non-device identity, bind the current mount in a separate worker manifest and retain both manifest hashes.

- #398 2026-11-09T03:25:44.000Z `release_risk_decisions_pending`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Two pre-release risk items need a user decision. Finding 4: the browser preview parses spreadsheets and CSV with xlsx 0.18.5 (CVE-2023-30533, CVE-2024-22363); the fixed SheetJS release is published only on the SheetJS CDN, so an upgrade means a new dependency source and npm install, while moving preview to the backend's existing pandas/openpyxl reader needs no new dependency but makes CSV preview require an open project. Finding 1 remainder: a time-trend chart still sends up to 400 row-level values to a remote provider; the default policy withholds rows, and sending summaries instead changes what chart Ask AI sees.; resolution: `open`; lesson: A dependency or default-sharing change that alters what users get is a decision to present, not a fix to slip in.
- #404 2026-11-09T11:40:00.000Z `did_footprint_above_v190`; cause_status: `suspected`; cause: Footprint gate (1468f315): DID candidate median 6.92% above v1.9.0. Diagnostics (NOT_ACCEPTANCE): both versions peak in the diagnostics-stage event study (linearmodels PanelOLS fit), plus the candidate's Stata-rule clustered_covariance pass. Event study alone on the captured 1M-row input, fresh processes after a warm-up: v1.9.0 median 952 MiB, candidate 975 (+2.4%). The rest (~45 MiB) is memory held before diagnostics, not yet attributed. Separate: both versions show occasional +350-650 MiB runs that stay high afterwards (v1.9.0 1565/1379, candidate 1384/1462, mostly a batch's first run); MallocLargeCache=0 raises every run to ~1450 MiB; PYTHONHASHSEED=0 is tight (944-977). A lifetime trim of clustered_covariance did not lower the traced peak and was reverted.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Attribute a memory ratio stage by stage before changing code; the whole-run gap mixed a small new-step cost with retained state and first-run outliers.
- #405 2026-11-09T13:30:00.000Z `did_footprint_variance_root_cause`; cause_status: `suspected`; cause: Root-cause round for DID footprint variance (NOT_ACCEPTANCE, HEAD a7bbb87a). Found: each linearmodels from_formula fit leaves formulaic's materialized data in reference cycles (1989-2318 unreachable objects; a 1M x 16 frame of 128 MiB, 13 MiB series, 3 PanelData; about 300 MiB), freed only by a full collection; malloc_zone_pressure_relief released nothing, so allocator retention was not the end-of-run difference. A fit_from_formula helper with gc.collect after each of the 6 fits kept results bit-identical but did not lower full-workflow peaks (8 runs 912-1202 MiB, later stages held more), so it was reverted; the attempt is kept under attempt-gc-after-formula-fit/. Fixed PYTHONHASHSEED=0 still varied (969-1103); native threads = 1 gave 993-1049, MallocMaxMagazines=1 979-1071, default 940-1103; the intermittent +300-650 MiB runs (about one in eight) did not occur in these small groups, so their source is still not isolated.; resolution: `open`; lesson: A memory gate with a 5% noise rule over 5 runs per lane cannot resolve a metric whose run-to-run spread in both versions is about 5% with one-in-eight outliers; isolating the source needs larger samples per condition.
- #412 2026-11-10T21:00:00.000Z `requests_per_task_diagnosis_corrected`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Requests per task (5 NVIDIA binary_logit positions, 26-40, v1.9.0 baseline 12): the 'proposal ready' texts after each proposal are server-written (stop_reason proposal_ready), not model requests, so an earlier estimate that batching around confirmations would save 5-8 requests was wrong. Real requests by class across ~186: repeated final summaries driven by user nudges ~30, run_operation argument/validation errors 19 (9 in the failed position), update_plan errors 10, ACTIVE_HEAD_MISMATCH after a refit 4, coefficient reads after a refit 12. Fixed: 30f49e7b a full-analysis answer without a completion contract now gets the bounded completion recovery instead of waiting for a nudge (5 of 10 nudges in passing positions; nudges are an M0.5 gate, the change does not by itself cut requests); 44bcce20 a confirmed refit's outcome carries its model_estimates (removes the 12 reads). Fixed prompt overhead is 44.7k tokens on the first request (system ~96k chars, 34 tool schemas ~69k chars); v1.9.0 itself used 982k prompt tokens per task, so the <=300k gate is not reachable by request cuts alone.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Count model requests from the provider tape, not assistant messages; server-written messages look like replies.
- #442 2026-11-18T13:18:36.204Z `m5_milestone_acceptance_remains_open`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The formal cohort preflight found less than 25 GiB free. The sealed holdout and external reference and judge lanes have separate preconditions.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Keep exact cohort definitions and family/truth scoring separate from the execution and sealed exposure receipts.
- #444 2026-11-18T13:18:36.204Z `nvidia_auto_sample_stopped_at_cap`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The fixed synthetic Auto panel task hit the recorded request cap before generating a plan or completing.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Do not extend a stopped sampling budget or infer whole-cohort quality from an unfinished fixed task.
- #445 2026-11-18T20:18:20.292Z `auto_planning_recovery_live_check`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Auto previously reached operation confirmation before planning. Successive recorded probes exposed project-denied plans, string-encoded preparation flags and an opaque workflow wrapper unable to cover actual steps.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Require planning before execution. Verify project denial, preparation-only semantics and actual workflow members before granting plan authority.
- #461 2026-11-20T02:21:33.840Z `m0_m5_user_scoring_and_admission`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The user approved 54 truth-applicable correctness positions and 63 first-run deliveries per mode, plus two independent NVIDIA free blind reviewers and separate adjudication. A prepared Ask proposal was mislabeled as executed, Auto DID exhausted recovery after blocked exploration, and Compare on import-only IV hid the registry family.; resolution: `open`; lesson: Check visible effect state against durable permissions and records. Exercise valid intake workflows with a registered family.
- #465 2026-11-20T09:48:47.813Z `v191_benchmark_retention`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Interactive and offline replay performance drivers retained complete projects per observation. APFS clone accounting repeated shared input allocations per path in historical directory totals.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Reserve one active benchmark tree, save all observations and complete logs, and publish representatives only after validation.
