- #468 2026-11-20T13:20:00.000Z `v191_release_with_unmet_preregistered_targets`; cause_status: `known`; cause: User instruction 2026-11-20: write the rule-change proposal, finish what can be finished, freeze v1.9.1 and release; live tests stay on the free NVIDIA route. Under the written rules, hard gates (full suites, lint, structure, devline, host full gate, bit-identical default path, offline safety tests, QRData replay) must pass on the freeze 93e6f216. The preregistered M5 targets (development completion, rubric mean, pass^3, holdout, external, replication, ablations), the zero-nudge floor, refusal rate, prompt size and cache targets were not measured or not met; they are released as unmet, not claimed, and moved to v1.9.2. No threshold value changed. The resource gate is judged by a bootstrap 95% CI of the median ratio instead of requiring calibration noise below 5%.; resolution: `accepted`; lesson: A gate whose noise exceeds its signal on the available host never closes; judge it by an interval, and freeze a version before its last tenth instead of adding scope.

## All waste

- #72 2026-10-20T16:05:50.248Z `review_common_driver`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The uncommitted isolated-review prototype directly streamed an adapter instead of using the already-required common Core phase driver.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Read the phase integration contract before adding any model-backed role. Keep requests and cumulative budgets in Core; pure review transitions can replay original events, while mutating phases remain reconciliation-only.
- #108 2026-10-21T18:49:09.000Z `unapproved_provider_calls_while_profiling`; cause_status: `known`; cause: To profile a real session turn offline, Claude replaced the model adapter in agent_routes; after the M1 refactor adapters are created through runtime_adapter, so two profiling turns reached DeepSeek.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Any offline experiment blocks the network at the HTTP layer first, so a missed patch fails instead of billing.
- #118 2026-10-22T11:29:54.000Z `round_cap_below_reservation_headroom`; cause_status: `known`; cause: I sized the per-mode caps from expected spend; the ledger reserves each request's worst case from body bytes (about CNY 1.33 at peak prices with reasoning pass-back), so the Ask cap stopped panel_fe mid-task and the Auto pass was stopped by hand.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Size a paid round's cap as expected spend plus one worst-case request reservation.
- #135 2026-10-22T20:53:43.000Z `free_route_outage_not_stopping_round`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The free-route guard only judged usage.credit: a gateway that could not be reached was retried by the product, and a 429 rate limit was reported as missing credit.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A round guard must name every way a provider can refuse service and stop on the first one that makes further tasks meaningless.
- #136 2026-10-23T03:26:19.000Z `free_route_interruptions_counted_per_round`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The free-route guard tolerated three transport interruptions per round; WorkBuddy Hy3 and Step-5-Preview sometimes send no first byte for 90 s on 160-430 KB requests, which the product correctly abandons.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Bound provider interruptions where they happen (per task) and end only that task; a round-level count turns one slow task into a missing round.
- #142 2026-10-25T10:28:18.000Z `eval_round_ran_unpinned_model`; cause_status: `known`; cause: The speedup round script unset WORKBENCH_AGENT_MODEL, so it used the provider's saved model, which the person had switched to workbuddy-ai/step-5-preview, instead of DeepSeek V4.1 Flash.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: Every evaluation script pins the model it means to measure and checks the first request's model before trusting the round.
- #242 2026-11-01T12:26:36.000Z `round_lost_to_network_outage`; cause_status: `known`; cause: A 15-minute network outage stopped every task; the runner retried each three times about 9 s apart and ran out of tasks.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A lost connection says nothing about the task: rounds wait for the endpoint instead of spending task attempts.
- #266 2026-11-03T18:07:28.000Z `ols_full_frame_fingerprint`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Two run_ols callers (the Agent's post-estimation refit, whose result is discarded, and the auto-to-OLS fallback) passed no dataset snapshot, so the fit canonicalized every cell of the whole frame, unused columns included; run_ols also copied the whole frame.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: An optional argument whose absence triggers an expensive fallback needs a check at every call site; added lint rule ols-call-dataset-snapshot.
- #396 2026-11-09T02:27:18.000Z `rerun_contract_showed_source_family_options`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Refitting model:ols_1 as logit, the Agent sent covariance at the top of changes (rejected: legacy Analysis Loop field mixed with model_type), then in model_options (rejected: logit declares no model_options). The model.rerun contract listed the legacy covariance control and the source OLS option vocabulary and did not say which target families take model_options.; resolution: `resolved`; lesson: A contract that shows the source family's options must say, per target family, whether options carry over.
- #402 2026-11-09T09:20:00.000Z `figure_reader_decoded_duplicate_row_list`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Restoring analysis_sample.row_set (70a3bc12) kept the 1M OLS analysis peak footprint (median 571 vs 574 MiB without it, 6 alternating runs each) but raised the figure delivery peak 574 -> 635 MiB: ensure_figures decoded both row lists though figures use row_order only. 7b375ff3 decodes model results for figures without the stored list; 6 runs after: delivery median 581 MiB, analysis 3.86 s (3.83 before, 3.70 without the list), run directory 77.6 MiB (62.5 without). Model JSON bytes unchanged.; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: A byte-identity restore should be measured for its readers, not just its writer.
- #416 2026-11-13T12:00:00.000Z `fixed_prompt_overhead_reduced`; cause_status: `known`; cause: Each request carried about 45k prompt tokens before any history: the protocol 93 KB (68 KB of it the step vocabulary) and 34 tool declarations 69 KB (run_operation 38 KB); fixed overhead was two thirds of a task's prompt, history one quarter. Fixed by measurement on the 843 recorded tasks: 28a0ffeb keeps field signatures inline only for the 42 steps written at least three times (98.8% of signature-step uses); the other 94 keep name, summary and Required, and the Agent reads their contract in one call (vocabulary 67,880 -> 53,028 chars). f23d35be sends run_operation without the seven data.* branches outside its own operation_id enum, which no call could select (37,072 -> 19,648 chars; validation still uses the complete declaration). Also e562f8da: unchosen primary candidates now get the bounded completion recovery (the open.r3 nudge). Replay of specific.r1 with identical replies: every request 33 KB smaller, first request -19.7%, whole task -13.9% (1.41M -> about 1.21M by the run's chars ratio). Not measured live. Not done, judged not worth the risk without live runs: trimming echoed proposals in history (about 4%), pruning null fields from refit estimates (0.5 KB each). Remaining nudges are the frozen runner's literal completion marker (2) and a deliberate stop after a step failed twice (1). Noted: tests/test_cli_agent.py broken_event_sink case failed once in three full agent runs and passed 6/6 alone (load-dependent timing).; resolution: `mitigated`; lesson: Measure which declarations are ever used before paying for them in every request; a schema branch outside its own enum is pure cost.

## Root causes and solutions

- `actor-independent-export-policy-boundary`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The script-only hook boundary did not cover dataset/report services, existing downloads or client-rendered SVG.; solution: `resolved`
- `admitted-proposal-must-execute`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: operation.multi_step proposals targeting a model node were admitted; workflow compilation at confirmation requires a dataset node and raised an unmapped validation error (HTTP 500), leaving the record pending.; solution: `resolved`
- `agent-check-denial-before-publishing-proposal`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The Agent published a pending workflow before checking the project deny rules, and recovery did not distinguish a matching refit from a forbidden new model specification.; solution: `resolved`
- `agent-eval-confirmation-denial-is-terminal-for-revision`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The simulated user continued to confirm an unchanged proposal after a definitive project permission denial.; solution: `resolved`
- `agent-seed-model-is-not-the-requested-analysis`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The session focus told the Agent the selected import node should be acted on, and nothing in the OLS result said that OLS does not serve a binary outcome, so the Agent read the seed OLS as the requested analysis and answered with a linear probability model.; solution: `mitigated`
- `approved-promises-require-producer-proof`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The retained approved Panel plan passed its family playbook while six criteria and nine expected outputs had no deterministic proof. The shared delivery check now preserves that playbook pass but marks overall partial; exact registered request and product proofs remain separate from execution receipts.; solution: `mitigated`
- `auto-planning-recovery-live-check`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Auto previously reached operation confirmation before planning. Successive recorded probes exposed project-denied plans, string-encoded preparation flags and an opaque workflow wrapper unable to cover actual steps.; solution: `mitigated`
- `benchmark-register-run-share-actual-environment`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The component runner pins the complete environment. Separate terminal invocations changed CODEX_TOOL_CALL_ID and the original launch refused before any product worker; source and numerical settings were unchanged.; solution: `resolved`
- `bind-what-the-server-knows`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: In the no-thinking round the weaker model miscopied a 64-hex source sha256 (one digit) and named a report that did not cover the selected results; the gate refused without saying what was wrong.; solution: `resolved`
- `block-network-in-offline-experiments`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: To profile a real session turn offline, Claude replaced the model adapter in agent_routes; after the M1 refactor adapters are created through runtime_adapter, so two profiling turns reached DeepSeek.; solution: `resolved`
- `bound-every-model-visible-message`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The orchestrator wrote each executed operation's whole result record as the model-visible text of its operation_result message; a Kaplan-Meier workflow result was ~450k characters (every curve point and censoring flag) and was resent on every later request and into compaction.; solution: `resolved`
- `bound-interruptions-per-task`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The free-route guard tolerated three transport interruptions per round; WorkBuddy Hy3 and Step-5-Preview sometimes send no first byte for 90 s on 160-430 KB requests, which the product correctly abandons.; solution: `resolved`
- `bounded-eval-capacity-and-local-failures-separate`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The frozen75dd continuation ended on a complete error-onlyNVIDIA429after the explicitly approved30shared recoveries. Runner peak9starts/60s is below40; the upstream account/capacity rule is unknown. Panel completed with29.41percent tool rejection; leverage remained incomplete; survival stopped. These local failures are separate from transport refusal.; solution: `open`
- `caller-type-errors-need-wire-grounded-origin`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The finalized frozen IV task sent nine malformed changes strings despite an object-only schema and six successful multi-step execution receipts. Twelve later public replies nevertheless blamed platform deserialization. The final required influence-sample comparison did not execute.; solution: `mitigated`
- `canonical-evidence-and-bounded-repair-contract`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Recorded development analysis copied a display result alias into a registered artifact slot, so the strict completion gate stayed partial while prose claimed question completion. Repeated string-shaped changes and hidden head/inspection errors prevented bounded repair. Unknown inspection errors could previously return an unchecked request.; solution: `mitigated`
- `cap-includes-reservation-headroom`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: I sized the per-mode caps from expected spend; the ledger reserves each request's worst case from body bytes (about CNY 1.33 at peak prices with reasoning pass-back), so the Ask cap stopped panel_fe mid-task and the Auto pass was stopped by hand.; solution: `resolved`
- `clock-gap-check-machine-sleep-first`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The lease and tape used the wall clock while the turn budget used the monotonic clock, and evaluation rounds did not hold the machine awake.; solution: `resolved`
- `closed-figure-buffers-and-default-path-measurement-fidelity`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Closed Matplotlib figures retain observation-sized coordinate buffers until cyclic collection; allocation snapshots identify approximately 132MB in line/copy/stack arrays. This does not yet explain every physical-RSS phase difference.; solution: `open`
- `complete-packets-and-preflight-require-real-evidence`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The review projection discarded real correlation/group lists yet claimed completeness; rerun declaration preflight swallowed unavailable source inputs before proposal admission. Both allowed a misleading success/readiness signal.; solution: `resolved`
- `completion-requirement-must-be-owned-and-satisfiable`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: An Agent-chosen estimand no producer can certify, receipts that covered only the first plan step, and one refused done claim discarding the primary choice.; solution: `resolved`
- `component-gains-do-not-close-absolute-default-gate`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Native rank kernels and owned two-lane scheduling improve the real default workflow, but end-to-end diagnostics, figures and identity work still exceed the 8 second objective. Component gains cannot close the absolute gate.; solution: `mitigated`
- `continuation-inherits-person-request`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Continuations set the command text to None, so language detection fell back to English, and publication prepended a notice to answers that already began with one.; solution: `resolved`
- `core-dispatch-command-identity-collision`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Initial invocation metadata used a new Core command even when the server dispatcher already owned a command; an existing dispatch proposal test exposed the mismatch.; solution: `resolved`
- `count-requests-from-the-tape`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Requests per task (5 NVIDIA binary_logit positions, 26-40, v1.9.0 baseline 12): the 'proposal ready' texts after each proposal are server-written (stop_reason proposal_ready), not model requests, so an earlier estimate that batching around confirmations would save 5-8 requests was wrong. Real requests by class across ~186: repeated final summaries driven by user nudges ~30, run_operation argument/validation errors 19 (9 in the failed position), update_plan errors 10, ACTIVE_HEAD_MISMATCH after a refit 4, coefficient reads after a refit 12. Fixed: 30f49e7b a full-analysis answer without a completion contract now gets the bounded completion recovery instead of waiting for a nudge (5 of 10 nudges in passing positions; nudges are an M0.5 gate, the change does not by itself cut requests); 44bcce20 a confirmed refit's outcome carries its model_estimates (removes the 12 reads). Fixed prompt overhead is 44.7k tokens on the first request (system ~96k chars, 34 tool schemas ~69k chars); v1.9.0 itself used 982k prompt tokens per task, so the <=300k gate is not reachable by request cuts alone.; solution: `mitigated`
- `declared-spec-beats-auto-detection`: occurrences=2; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Routing overrides auto-detected panel columns only for the column the user or Agent supplied; with entity_col set and time_col unset, the auto-detected time column (year) still reaches PanelOLS, which adds TimeEffects.; solution: `open`
- `default-gate-optional-evidence`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The v6 default gate incorrectly required the optional advanced statistics artifact for registered DID, panel, and Cox workloads. The worker also classified a statistics capture failure as an inference-kernel failure.; solution: `resolved`
- `default-path-evidence-resource-budget`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `suspected`; root cause: Original default completion JSON expanded observation arrays and repeated source decoding; IV also ran exact AR inversion unconditionally. Those costs are removed in the candidate, but paired panel elapsed and DID RSS still fail and remain unattributed.; solution: `open`
- `demo-with-real-turn-in-visible-ui`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The attachment display was shown with a message written directly into a session, while the real model check ran in another project the user was not looking at.; solution: `resolved`
- `denial-recovery-names-permitted-target`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: At 7821c4c7 binary_logit.open.r3 proposed model.genesis (Logit with age and urban controls), which the project denies. The recovery told it to refit only a model whose predictors match the intended specification, without naming the model node; one contract inspection on stage:cleaned failed, and the Agent ran only proportion, chi-square and Mann-Whitney tests. The gate's no_eligible_primary repair listed no candidates and did not name the imported OLS that model.rerun could refit with Logit. It stopped on nudge_budget after 40 requests.; solution: `resolved`
- `derive-performance-metadata-from-raw-medians`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The preceding gate event transcribed an overprecise derived ratio incorrectly. Recompute directly from the unchanged report medians; raw observations,14.3x rounded report, numerical equality and every gate are unchanged.; solution: `resolved`
- `diagnostic-risk-needs-faithful-bounded-model-view`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The persisted native diagnostic had Cook and high-influence facts, but inspection exposed only preview highlights and omitted the risk section. Raw wire confirmed the facts were absent; later completion prose exceeded the question-gate verification scope.; solution: `open`
- `diagnostics-share-the-model-sample`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Diagnostics refused any missing value while OLS drops those rows by default, with a message that named no column.; solution: `resolved`
- `drain-owned-effects-before-releasing-cancelled-leases`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Cancelling the outer lifecycle task while awaiting asyncio.to_thread cancelled only the await; finally released the domain lease while its native worker still ran and its effect was not bound.; solution: `resolved`
- `durable-task-and-review-authority`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Agent plan prose could erase full-analysis or estimand requirements after continuation; unchanged-question form edits could reset them. Reviewer packets omitted frequency aggregates and reused stale task or criteria judgments. Binding true entry provenance exposed missing session identity allocations in strict replay.; solution: `mitigated`
- `durable-turns-need-operation-reconciliation`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: On-disk audit found JSON event polling rather than SSE, reset-only core budgets, no startup recovery, and Notebook execution paths with independent admission authority.; solution: `open`
- `effects-vs-structure`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: c11a1a6d stopped adding time effects for entity-only panel fits but also dropped the time index, so panel diagnostics saw n_times = nobs and no time pairing.; solution: `resolved`
- `entry-size-is-not-extension-cost`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Pure extraction keeps the existing separate declaration, semantic validation, column-resolution and execution branches.; solution: `open`
- `enumerate-production-build-variants`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The aggregate build/check enumerated components, but did not include the production AR moments variant. Its successful exit did not certify that variant.; solution: `resolved`
- `eval-orphan-process-loss-requires-explicit-resumption`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: Fresh frozen cohort launcher and preview processes were absent; no final task record or response was persisted for one reserved request. Existing evidence does not identify an upstream error or process termination cause.; solution: `open`
- `eval-round-pins-model`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The speedup round script unset WORKBENCH_AGENT_MODEL, so it used the provider's saved model, which the person had switched to workbuddy-ai/step-5-preview, instead of DeepSeek V4.1 Flash.; solution: `resolved`
- `eval-wall-limit-excludes-harness-pacing`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The evaluator's 1,800 s per-task limit counted the waits the harness itself inserts between NVIDIA starts; at 75 s pacing most of the single run's 1,285 s was pacing, so the limit measured the route's rate rather than the Agent.; solution: `resolved`
- `evaluation-reference-not-progress-pointer`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Failure event60a73f72-e5ac-4a73-bc87-bd2c53c0bacf accidentally used the mutable progress checklist as its evidence reference. This correction points to the full tracked evaluation report; the original failure and every raw outcome remain unchanged.; solution: `resolved`
- `every-agent-operation-has-a-direct-route`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: OLS clustered standard errors need a cluster column, but model.genesis forbids entity_col for OLS and OLS model_options have no cluster field, so the Agent cannot cluster an OLS fit by classroom (baseline cluster_rct.specific, the STAR replication). The fix belongs in contracts/model, a protected path of this line reserved for the v1.9.2 inference kernel.; solution: `open`
- `every-missing-item-has-an-actionable-repair`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: At 428ee909 binary_logit.open.r1 bound Logit primary, Probit sensitivity, margins and a report, then looped on outcome_family:diagnostics:computed_and_model_bound: the Agent named diagnostics_logit_1, and derive_evidence skipped the primary's own checks_logit_1 whenever any same-run diagnostic was named. The evidence catalog did not list check records (diagnostics_meet_requirements false) and evaluate emitted no repair step, so each recovery nudge was unactionable.; solution: `resolved`
- `exact-moment-ar-noisy-calibration-not-production-acceptance`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: Exact-moment AR retains the original input and complete proof, but calibration and full-process time noise exceed the unchanged five-percent gate. The observed large improvement does not authorize production switching.; solution: `open`
- `expensive-default-needs-call-site-check`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Two run_ols callers (the Agent's post-estimation refit, whose result is discarded, and the auto-to-OLS fallback) passed no dataset snapshot, so the fit canonicalized every cell of the whole frame, unused columns included; run_ols also copied the whole frame.; solution: `resolved`
- `explicit-sample-count-bypassed-numeric-check`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: All small integers and year-shaped integers were exempted without checking whether they stated a sample count.; solution: `resolved`
- `fast-iv-exclusive-abort`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Free storage fell below the required floor before the second worker. The first worker's host monitor also recorded a concurrent progress inspection.; solution: `mitigated`
- `fast-iv-input-device-pin`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The IV acceptance driver copied a registered device number after it changed, and the worker rejected the stale file identity despite a matching input SHA-256.; solution: `resolved`
- `fast-iv-worker-build-receipt`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The optional-IV performance gate pinned disk build metadata without verifying the native build receipt persisted by each measured worker.; solution: `resolved`
- `fingerprint-every-dtype`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The compact completion-evidence frame fingerprint hashed numpy column buffers; datetime64 and timedelta64 columns expose no buffer.; solution: `resolved`
- `fingerprint-speedup-is-not-end-to-end-acceptance`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: Candidate perf_1m printed ingest plus OLS at 29.05 seconds, above the approved 8-second objective despite the decimal sample-fingerprint microbenchmark improvement. Remaining end-to-end cost needs profiling.; solution: `open`
- `fixed-input-digest-versus-measurement-local-file-identity`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The canonical fixed input digest was valid, but a historical device identifier was reused as a per-run identity. The first preflight correctly refused before any worker; why the filesystem device id changed is not established.; solution: `resolved`
- `forecast-metric-complement-misdiagnosis`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The investigator initially treated interval coverage and separately configured lower-quantile exceptions as complementary events.; solution: `resolved`
- `forest-variable-owner-binding`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Unindexed variables from different datasets shared a bare column-name key in the project forest. Incoming role edges were attached to the wrong visual branch.; solution: `resolved`
- `freeze-and-interval-gates`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: User instruction 2026-11-20: write the rule-change proposal, finish what can be finished, freeze v1.9.1 and release; live tests stay on the free NVIDIA route. Under the written rules, hard gates (full suites, lint, structure, devline, host full gate, bit-identical default path, offline safety tests, QRData replay) must pass on the freeze 93e6f216. The preregistered M5 targets (development completion, rubric mean, pass^3, holdout, external, replication, ablations), the zero-nudge floor, refusal rate, prompt size and cache targets were not measured or not met; they are released as unmet, not claimed, and moved to v1.9.2. No threshold value changed. The resource gate is judged by a bootstrap 95% CI of the median ratio instead of requiring calibration noise below 5%.; solution: `accepted`
- `fresh-tasks-did-not-replicate-timeout-gain`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: On 15 unseen QRData tasks, c0693c47 scored 8 and 79dc2aae scored 9 (McNemar p=1). The one flip (354) came from the model choosing a different specification, not from the fixes.; solution: `open`
- `gate-and-report-share-evidence-definition`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: At b25d3a1b binary_logit.open.r1 met the outcome_family model, diagnostics and sensitivity requirements, then looped on report:registered_nonempty: the gate binds computed results (statistical_exploration) and requires the report to cover them, but report_sources accepted only diagnostic artifact types, so the gate's own compose_report failed and narrower reports did not count. The 128 evidence rows were also first-come, which would have left the marginal effects out of the report.; solution: `resolved`
- `gate-asks-only-for-decisions`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The completion gate required the Agent to copy exact run/artifact ids for the primary model, its companion diagnostics, alternative fits and a covering report, and its catalog scanned only the source run, so fits in branch runs were invisible.; solution: `resolved`
- `gate-requirement-satisfiable-for-every-data-shape`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The completion gate required a high-influence sample comparison but offered a refit only when a unique identifying column existed; without one no option was offered and the model could not construct it.; solution: `resolved`
- `gate-tests-use-real-fit-products`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: In the 501cdda6 round binary_logit.open.r1 fitted Logit by model.rerun and a Probit comparison, but the gate kept outcome_family:sensitivity:distinct_point_estimation missing: logit and probit model_result payloads carry no point_estimation_fingerprint, so no comparison could ever be distinct. The repair step pointed at prepared options that were model.genesis (refused there); the Agent never composed a report and repeated one partial answer through 15 recovery prompts until the nudge budget. open.r2 was stopping the same way and the user stopped the round.; solution: `resolved`
- `governed-extension-receipts-and-discovery`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Offline review reproduced incomplete receipt publication and missing exceptional after-operation events; integration exposed missing manual catalog words and a pre-extension capability count.; solution: `resolved`
- `grade-against-the-draw`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The leverage scenario scored estimates against the population slope 1.0, but the goal variant's fixed draw supports 1.276 on its clean rows, so no correct analysis could pass.; solution: `accepted`
- `guard-names-every-refusal`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The free-route guard only judged usage.credit: a gateway that could not be reached was retried by the product, and a 429 rate limit was reported as missing credit.; solution: `resolved`

- `macos-peak-memory-metric`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The intermittent +400-1100 MiB footprint runs (in both versions; mostly the first run after another heavy workload) are memory compression, not program memory. With every run directory under a Spotlight-excluded .noindex folder, the same sequence gave 2060-2319 MiB, compressed 0-17, no outlier (9 runs each). Host: 24 GiB, 3.5 GiB free, 5.8 GiB compressor, mds_stores 1.3 GiB; each measured run writes about 200 MB that Spotlight then indexes.; solution: `mitigated`
- `measure-overhead-on-long-real-sessions`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Session lookups (get_entry, get_branch, append-once checks, transcript projection) re-read and re-parsed the whole JSONL log on every call, and navigation listed all operation records and re-read the event log once per message; cost grew with the square of the conversation length.; solution: `resolved`

Also e562f8da: unchosen primary candidates now get the bounded completion recovery (the open.r3 nudge). Replay of specific.r1 with identical replies: every request 33 KB smaller, first request -19.7%, whole task -13.9% (1.41M -> about 1.21M by the run's chars ratio). Not measured live. Not done, judged not worth the risk without live runs: trimming echoed proposals in history (about 4%), pruning null fields from refit estimates (0.5 KB each). Remaining nudges are the frozen runner's literal completion marker (2) and a deliberate stop after a step failed twice (1). Noted: tests/test_cli_agent.py broken_event_sink case failed once in three full agent runs and passed 6/6 alone (load-dependent timing).; solution: `mitigated`
- `measure-readers-of-restored-fields`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Restoring analysis_sample.row_set (70a3bc12) kept the 1M OLS analysis peak footprint (median 571 vs 574 MiB without it, 6 alternating runs each) but raised the figure delivery peak 574 -> 635 MiB: ensure_figures decoded both row lists though figures use row_order only. 7b375ff3 decodes model results for figures without the stored list; 6 runs after: delivery median 581 MiB, analysis 3.86 s (3.83 before, 3.70 without the list), run directory 77.6 MiB (62.5 without). Model JSON bytes unchanged.; solution: `mitigated`
- `measurement-cli-must-propagate-failed-gates`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The offline pipeline driver wrote a failed noise gate report but returned None from main, so its actual process exit was zero. The original failed report and measurements were retained and were never declared full acceptance.; solution: `resolved`
- `memory-gap-attribution`: occurrences=2; cause_status: `suspected`; root cause: Footprint gate (1468f315): DID candidate median 6.92% above v1.9.0. Diagnostics (NOT_ACCEPTANCE): both versions peak in the diagnostics-stage event study (linearmodels PanelOLS fit), plus the candidate's Stata-rule clustered_covariance pass. Event study alone on the captured 1M-row input, fresh processes after a warm-up: v1.9.0 median 952 MiB, candidate 975 (+2.4%). The rest (~45 MiB) is memory held before diagnostics, not yet attributed. Separate: both versions show occasional +350-650 MiB runs that stay high afterwards (v1.9.0 1565/1379, candidate 1384/1462, mostly a batch's first run); MallocLargeCache=0 raises every run to ~1450 MiB; PYTHONHASHSEED=0 is tight (944-977). A lifetime trim of clustered_covariance did not lower the traced peak and was reverted.; solution: `open`
- `memory-sample-long-runs`: occurrences=2; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: The system low-memory dialog showed Claude and its children at 21.04 GB while the 40-task round ran; it had fallen to 3.5 GB by the first check. Live processes never peaked above 1.3 GB, so the holder exited unobserved. A replay of the one suspect run peaked at 0.35 GB. Separately macOS refuses RLIMIT_AS, so sandboxed code had no effective memory limit.; solution: `resolved`
- `merge-whole-function-contract`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: A partial merge dropped the return in the cast source resolver, causing callers to unpack None. The final merge retains the complete resolver from the first parent.; solution: `resolved`
- `missing-requirement-needs-executable-step`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The completion gate named a missing distinct-point-estimation or influence-sample comparison but gave no executable next step, and its next_step asked for the report first.; solution: `resolved`
- `missing-value-policy-per-role`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: linearmodels' formula interface raises on a missing outcome, endogenous or instrument value (it drops a missing exogenous one) and the product never dropped incomplete rows before an IV fit, so every IV run on such data stopped at estimation.; solution: `resolved`
- `model-rerun-public-schema-full-gate`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Two route assertions still required server-owned pins in the model-facing rerun schema.; solution: `mitigated`
- `native-component-noise-before-integration`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Baseline-only whole-worker RSS variation was 5.273364 percent in five calibrations, exceeding the unchanged less-than-five-percent requirement. The observed paired medians therefore do not certify native acceleration.; solution: `open`
- `native-diagnostic-recovery-needs-exact-companion`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Any same-run diagnostic suppressed the exact native model companion; AR recovery was absent from default operation digests and generic criteria guidance overwrote specific next steps.; solution: `mitigated`
- `native-library-in-place-publication`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Copying a rebuilt Rust dylib over a previously mapped inode caused macOS to terminate a fresh loader process. The signed Cargo target with the same contents loaded successfully.; solution: `resolved`
- `native-research-component-noise`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The native local-level per-call warm duration varied above the five-percent ceiling in the first matched comparison.; solution: `open`
- `native-speed-does-not-offset-memory-or-observer-noise-failure`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `suspected`; root cause: The production moments public API preserves original-input exact certificates and is 14.4 times faster in the fixed component lane, but RSS median grows 8.4 percent and RSS/process noise exceed five percent. Repeated parsing of the large software-registration tree adds known Python allocations; the original RSS difference has not yet been attributed to a phase.; solution: `open`
- `native-speedup-component-evidence-is-not-default-acceptance`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Native AR and large canonical encoding show exact component benefits; corrected Agent replay has stable local gains but whole-worker and process noise exceed five percent, associated with imports. Default workflow and integer-rank coverage still pending.; solution: `open`
- `new-playbook-must-reach-every-consumer`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: At aeb81ade the method check withdrew the imported-OLS draft and the Agent went straight to model.rerun as Logit, computed marginal effects and a Probit comparison, but the turn ended on KeyError 'outcome_family': the partial-note labels were a literal per-playbook table. The gate's ready report.compose also named checks_logit_1, which report sources refused, costing one failed report.; solution: `resolved`
- `new-run-path-must-keep-nodes-addressable`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The import-only path recorded graph nodes but never wrote node_index.json, so node tools refused them as CONTEXT_STALE.; solution: `resolved`
- `nvidia-auto-sample-stopped-at-cap`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The fixed synthetic Auto panel task hit the recorded request cap before generating a plan or completing.; solution: `open`
- `nvidia-named-tool-schema-rejected`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The official NVIDIA Ultra endpoint rejects the named tool grammar when the plan JSON Schema includes not, although the same tool declaration works in automatic mode.; solution: `mitigated`
- `nvidia-smoke-unknown-usage-not-capability-verdict`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: DeepSeekV4.1Flash and GLM5.3Flash each received one authorized request attempt but produced no HTTP response within60seconds. Usage and the provider-side cause remain unknown; neither was retried.; solution: `open`
- `nvidia-sse-error-before-local-close-classification`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: User approved preserving the first unknown attempt and starting the repaired candidate. IV Ask exhausted eight nudges with no IV model and eight of fifteen tools refused. Panel Ask later received a complete SSE error frame with provider code503 and no usage; early error-path close was taped as CancelledError. No retry or later task was started.; solution: `open`
- `objective-and-regression-gates-remain-distinct`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The prior resource-regression acceptance did not certify the objective OLS 8-second target or all analysis/skill/browser evaluations. The default OLS synchronous contract also delays cancellation.; solution: `open`
- `ols-row-order-lossless-archive`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Legacy OLS results store sample order and a sorted copy of the same identifiers, and artifact consumers verify the original JSON bytes.; solution: `mitigated`
- `oracle-the-consumer-path`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The workflow adapter for iv.gmm and iv.weak_instruments passes the bound exog columns without an intercept, unlike the diagnostics and GLM-extension adapters; the pack oracle tests supply an explicit constant, so the consumer path was never compared with an oracle.; solution: `open`
- `owned-async-execution-must-reconcile-original-receipt`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The common Auto/headless proposal runner returned after a single async rerun submit. Unlike the manual UI it never observed completion through the existing parent reconciler; two retained child runs completed while their parent receipts stayed running.; solution: `resolved`
- `paid-evaluation-truth-and-typed-evidence-binding`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Paid development replay exposed ambiguous primary evidence, wrong report refs, missing Agent AR reachability and repeated partial completion. A ConnectTimeout ended the last exchange without usage; the archived USD-times-8 estimate did not bound current CNY prices.; solution: `open`
- `partial-sse-eof-needs-terminal-and-usage-evidence`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: HTTP200 returned partial public content then EOF without finish_reason, DONE or usage accounting; upstream or intermediary truncation source unproven.; solution: `open`
- `patched-private-signatures-and-full-suite`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The four full-suite failures found in the review of fa70e117 are fixed: the statistics-stage bit-identity harness takes the new native_math._pair_ranks signature and the guard runs and passes again; the capability pin counts prediction.native_forest_apply (181 capabilities, 0 gaps); the forest-apply description fits the 90-character limit. Full backend suite: 14,061 passed, 0 failed. The three design concerns of that review (forced recovery on focused questions, no inline step contracts, request admission margin) are unchanged and wait for the user's decision.; solution: `resolved`
- `per-step-readers-use-log-index`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: tool_receipts.read re-read and strictly parsed the whole session log and then parsed it again via get_branch, about three times per tool call.; solution: `resolved`
- `plan-authority-ignored-dependency-readiness`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Plan scopes unioned every open step without dependency readiness; a request could gain plan authority before its prerequisite completed.; solution: `resolved`
- `plan-authority-patches-preserve-limits-and-empty-intent`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Replacing a partial budget erased other approved caps; explicit narrower or empty permission classes were ignored. Finite steps changed to accepted zero meant unlimited without reopening approval.; solution: `resolved`
- `preheader-protocol-disconnection-needs-explicit-recovery-contract`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: HTTP transport raised RemoteProtocolError: Server disconnected without sending a response. No upstream headers or body were received. Ultimate upstream or intermediary source is not identified.; solution: `open`
- `preserve-entry-initialization-order-during-extraction`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Moving code with filtered entry imports changed initialization order and removed an import-registered workflow declaration.; solution: `resolved`
- `preview-origin-before-key-handoff`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The agent-created backend preview lacked the configured frontend5195 origin, so the real browser proxy could read without an Origin header in the earlier check but refused browser-origin requests. The provider dropdown also lacked an NVIDIA preset.; solution: `resolved`
- `primary-oracle-chain-before-derived-summary`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: The historical derived Stata JSON summary does not match its recorded manifest digest, including in the original archive commit. The reason is not established.; solution: `open`
- `probe-control-combinations`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: c49d247b expressed no thinking as a reasoning_effort of none beside a disabled thinking switch; the Magpie gateway thinks whenever the reasoning_effort field is present, whatever its value.; solution: `resolved`
- `provider-editor-catalog-density-and-identity`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Model mappings used intrinsic-minimum fractional columns and unconstrained preformatted JSON; model ID was also part of the React row key, remounting focused inputs on edits. Plain model labels became unreadable with18 entries; initial large tiles were rejected for low density.; solution: `resolved`
- `provider-local-state-and-remote-timeout-boundaries`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: Provider cards cached the on-mount snapshot and retained a model-specific provider name. That local UI issue is fixed. Separately, two NVIDIA endpoints still returned no response headers after complete TLS/request send, including minimal requests without tools; remote processing or return-path cause is unresolved.; solution: `mitigated`
- `provider-queue-counts-inside-original-command-budget`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The fresh two-task cohort used one serialized HTTP lease. Panel spent340.683 seconds queued within its original600 second Ask command budget; its in-flight request was cancelled at that local deadline with unknown usage. IV then observed the shared stop. This directly localizes the cancellation and does not prove an upstream provider failure.; solution: `open`
- `provider-queue-wait-cross-layer-contract`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: NVIDIA configured waits were truncated independently by HTTP silence30s and Agent idle90s; timeout alone does not identify remote availability or congestion.; solution: `mitigated`
- `provider-refusal-is-not-zero-usage`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `external`; root cause: The pinned free gateway returned HTTP403 on the first IV request, reporting an account restriction. No usage was returned; the guard stopped the batch without retry or model substitution.; solution: `open`
- `provider-specific-none-control-boundary`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The shared request builder encoded every declared none effort as DeepSeek thinking disabled. The NVIDIA documented standard reasoning_effort none was absent in the captured request; the new regression test failed before the minimal correction.; solution: `resolved`
- `read-confirm-status-in-loops`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Column references were checked only at confirmation, where the validation error escaped as HTTP 500. The record stayed pending while the continuation note told the Agent the step had run, so it searched for results and proposed the step again. All 12 stuck operations were in the 5 tasks that timed out.; solution: `mitigated`
- `read-the-writer-before-analysis`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: I treated the tape's 'at' field as the response end time; recorder.py writes the request start time.; solution: `resolved`
- `readerror-before-headers-remains-outside-503-permission`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: The resumed fixed panel task produced three non-seed model artifacts, then its37th exchange raised ReadError before response headers after46.867 seconds. No response bytes or usage were received. This is outside the permitted complete503-only recovery and the guard correctly stopped. Offline IV recording also exposed general contract/feedback gaps; those are repaired without changing the frozen live product.; solution: `mitigated`
- `real-turn-before-claiming-display`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Display work passed unit tests but a real turn showed raw result JSON filling the panel, Esc wired only to the old composer, and no note after a stop. The tests exercised components the live workspace does not use, or scalar-only fixtures.; solution: `open`
- `receipt-backed-plan-dependencies`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: A subset operation receipt marked an entire declared multi-operation plan step done. Independent replay also showed an Agent could erase pending work or clear a downstream dependency without human reapproval.; solution: `mitigated`
- `refactoring-needs-extension-cost-evidence`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Mechanical extraction improves ownership but does not remove the two production files and four implementation locations required by the fixed generic statistical-family extension probe. Baseline was reconstructed after extraction from immutable 4abbeac0.; solution: `open`
- `registry-built-at-import-order`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Importing the report service while the tool registry loaded built the workflow step registry before imputation methods registered.; solution: `resolved`
- `release-eval-admission`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The retained candidate grading and original draft differ in pass-cubed applicability. The current aggregate drops missing external outcomes.; solution: `open`
- `removing-timeouts-does-not-prove-accuracy`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The 40-task round on 938c4527 removed pending operations (12 to 0) and timeouts (7 to 2), but tasks that used to time out now answer wrongly; 30/40 both rounds, McNemar p=1.; solution: `open`
- `replay-gates-on-recordings`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The focused-question gate rejected workflow capability results as computed results and never bound results the Agent did not name, so answers computed from the data were marked not tied to a result.; solution: `resolved`
- `replay-under-the-recorded-setup`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Tool-call errors in the 5 NVIDIA binary_logit positions (~29): fixed the general ones without loosening approvals or validation: 152c8319 update_plan without steps keeps the current steps (1); a7a10168 a completion source named by its dataset node binds that node's registered dataset, without reading data (2); f9cd1245 the refit outcome names the conversation head and the exact model_ref/model_run_id fields (1 ACTIVE_HEAD_MISMATCH proposal + 3 reads, 2 more wrong-field proposals). Not fixed: broken JSON in changes (4, model output), depends_on on earlier executed steps (3; validator has no session state and report.compose required_steps would still fail), AME/alias refusals (correct). Replay finding: agent_eval_run wrote the M5 deny rule only for held-out/formal runs, so dev-round replays diverged at the first denial (looked like a denied genesis becoming a proposal; the product still denies it); e83e9c7b adds --project-deny-policy. With it, goal/open.r1/open.r2 replay unchanged, specific.r1 request 3 now succeeds, completion recoveries replace nudges (open.r1 2, open.r2 1, open.r3 4). Earlier dev replays without the rule were not evidence past the denial. Pre-existing: tests/test_agent_iv_ar_operation.py stale_foreign cases fail when selected without the rest of the module (module-scoped fixture order), also at 137def82.; solution: `mitigated`
- `rerun-contract-target-family-options`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Refitting model:ols_1 as logit, the Agent sent covariance at the top of changes (rejected: legacy Analysis Loop field mixed with model_type), then in model_options (rejected: logit declares no model_options). The model.rerun contract listed the legacy covariance control and the source OLS option vocabulary and did not say which target families take model_options.; solution: `resolved`
- `retain-local-chain-lock-through-native-effect-lifetime`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The file lock covered only JSON acquisition, so a different execution key could take an expired lease while a native thread or process still owned its effects. A fresh same-key object could also release the active JSON.; solution: `resolved`
- `risk-fix-needs-user-decision`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Two pre-release risk items need a user decision. Finding 4: the browser preview parses spreadsheets and CSV with xlsx 0.18.5 (CVE-2023-30533, CVE-2024-22363); the fixed SheetJS release is published only on the SheetJS CDN, so an upgrade means a new dependency source and npm install, while moving preview to the backend's existing pandas/openpyxl reader needs no new dependency but makes CSV preview require an open project. Finding 1 remainder: a time-trend chart still sends up to 400 row-level values to a remote provider; the default policy withholds rows, and sending summaries instead changes what chart Ask AI sees.; solution: `open`
- `round-waits-out-outages`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: A 15-minute network outage stopped every task; the runner retried each three times about 9 s apart and ran out of tasks.; solution: `resolved`
- `same-request-contract-reuse-and-faithful-scientific-evidence-scope`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: One complete contract result was carried in thirty-three later wire requests although exact schema fields already existed in each request. The AR public view prioritized a large proof before set meaning, first-stage structured fields were absent from its summary, and broad no-rerun/numeric-check wording overstated restrictions and verification scope.; solution: `mitigated`
- `sharp-ceiling-check-own-bound-first`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The evaluation recorder read each streamed reply whole before passing it on, so the product's 90 s no-progress bound measured total generation time; answers longer than 90 s were cut. I attributed the resulting Hy3 and Step-5 'stalls' to the WorkBuddy route and changed the guard and retry policy around them.; solution: `resolved`
- `signed-evidence-and-role-binding`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Magnitude-only grounding hid reversed effects and the public result summary hid the actual proportion denominator and source.; solution: `mitigated`
- `stable-published-schema-order-across-interpreter-restarts`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: A real third-step killed-process recovery refused the tool contract because a set-derived operation enum changed order across Python hash seeds.; solution: `resolved`
- `strict-replay-needs-new-business-identities`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: New proposals and plans generate business IDs and timestamps that are included in model-visible messages and later strict requests. A pre-turn copy freezes existing state but cannot freeze newly generated identities.; solution: `open`
- `supervisor-io-failure-needs-owned-child-stop`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Local ENOSPC prevented supervisor heartbeat and emergency stop lock creation; supervisor exited120 while its child remained alive. Root later persisted stop and drained the existing request.; solution: `mitigated`
- `task-scope-and-producer-evidence-before-success`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Raw tapes and registered products reveal focused answers suppressed by full-analysis delivery, import blocked by seed OLS, incorrect frequency units and unverified simplex convergence. Causal coefficients also substituted for requested estimands.; solution: `mitigated`
- `task-terminal-provider-tail-and-turn-lease-must-be-reconciled`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `unknown`; root cause: The last request recorded upstream HTTP200, partial public text and no finish/DONE/usage, then ReadTimeout. The task record reports nudge_budget before the provider capture settles; the retained log also shows a fenced or expired turn lease. The causal ordering and ownership cleanup remain unproven.; solution: `open`
- `transcript-prose-must-not-compete-with-navigation-width`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Persisted assistant prose, navigation badges and fork actions competed as flex siblings; three real assistant message bodies measured zero width.; solution: `resolved`
- `typed-iv-ar-completion-reuses-actual-producer-and-original-approval`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The real fitted IV definition was hashed then discarded from completion products, and typed criteria did not support the existing AR producer. Valid human-confirmed products therefore remained unavailable to machine deliverable verification; generic recovery wording also conflated Ask confirmation with Auto plan approval.; solution: `resolved`
- `ultra-finish-is-not-final-usage`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: User explicitly permitted both evaluation batches named data transfer, resolving prior egress approval gate. The first IV Ask request streamed public content and a tool request; local finish_reason handling closed the response before final usage, so the recorder retained CancelledError and stopped the round. This is not provider unavailability.; solution: `mitigated`
- `ultra-whitespace-generation-needs-bounded-output-and-honest-length`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Observed HTTP200 SSE repeatedly emitted public JSON-repair narration and whitespace without finish or usage. The containing 600-second turn cancelled it. Default output cap was unstated and blank data refreshed idle progress; offline follow-up adds bounded defaults and honest nonexecuting length failure.; solution: `open`
- `v191-benchmark-retention`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Interactive and offline replay performance drivers retained complete projects per observation. APFS clone accounting repeated shared input allocations per path in historical directory totals.; solution: `mitigated`
- `v191-notebook-mixed-model-artifacts`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The planner computed required artifacts from every model step. The Notebook write seam checked those requirements against the single displayed capability, rejecting a valid mixed OLS and IV plan.; solution: `resolved`
- `v191-nvidia-browser-routes`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The built-in browser recorded real runner outcomes. A prepared Ask proposal was mislabeled as executed, Auto DID exhausted recovery after blocked exploration, and Compare on import-only IV hid the registry family.; solution: `open`
- `validate-citations-by-target`: occurrences=2; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The review parser accepts an evidence_ref only when it equals a packet key; the reviewer cites paths inside keys (plan.criteria[2], diagnostic:0.summary...).; solution: `open`
- `vector-interface-is-not-performance-evidence`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: Pandas label Index conversion and lookup added allocation compared with the existing row-id map. Fixed-input comparison showed slower median time and RSS beyond the original resource cap; timing noise also failed.; solution: `resolved`
- `verify-provider-protocol-on-the-wire`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The LLM client discarded DeepSeek reasoning_content although the thinking-mode guide requires it on later requests that carry tools; the API tolerated the omission, so nothing failed visibly.; solution: `resolved`
- `verify-the-instrument-before-the-round`: occurrences=1; cause_status: `known`; root cause: The evaluation recorder passed the provider response on with its content-encoding header after reading the decoded body, so the client decoded it a second time, failed, and the adapter resent requests that had succeeded (three identical requests per step in the pilot).; solution: `resolved`
