## Future guidance

- A byte-identity restore should be measured for its readers, not just its writer.
- A capability the Agent cannot name in a workflow does not exist for it, even when the UI form can do it.
- A changed private signature needs a search of the tests that patch it; run the full suite before calling a batch green.
- A clean transport EOF is not a complete model response. Preserve partial output and the unsettled usage reservation; it cannot inherit pre-header retry permission or be counted as successful analysis.
- A continuation inherits the person's last request; published notices replace, not stack.
- A contract that shows the source family's options must say, per target family, whether options carry over.
- A denial's recovery and the gate's repair must name the permitted next operation and its exact target: the existing model node and the estimator change, with the blocked extra predictors stated as a limitation.
- A dependency or default-sharing change that alters what users get is a decision to present, not a fix to slip in.
- A diagnostic of a model must use the sample that model used; dropping rows must be visible in its result.
- A gate must ask the model only for decisions it alone can make; bookkeeping that follows from verified products belongs to the server.
- A gate requirement must be satisfiable for every dataset shape it can fire on; when the server cannot build the step, say why instead of repeating the requirement.
- A gate whose noise exceeds its signal on the available host never closes; judge it by an interval, and freeze a version before its last tenth instead of adding scope.
- A gate's refusal is also a claim to the reader; replay it over recorded sessions, in both directions, before and after changing it.
- A live label without a fresh observer heartbeat is not evidence of a running worker. Preserve orphan reservations and unknown usage; process loss is not an eligible pre-header ReadError and must not inherit its retry permission.
- A lost connection says nothing about the task: rounds wait for the endpoint instead of spending task attempts.
- A measurement CLI must propagate failed gates as a nonzero process status. Preserve the exact measured driver and raw report when repairing reporting semantics after measurement.
- A memory gate with a 5% noise rule over 5 runs per lane cannot resolve a metric whose run-to-run spread in both versions is about 5% with one-in-eight outliers; isolating the source needs larger samples per condition.
- A new playbook value must be exercised through every consumer of the playbook name, the person-facing partial note and the report included; offline tests stopped at evaluate and missed both. Tests now iterate over the contract enum.
- A new run path must pass the same addressability check as the full pipeline, and a round's refusal reasons must be read while it runs, not only its score.
- A persistent request guard cannot replace OS child ownership when disk writes fail. Preserve the original IO failure and stop owned children if emergency stop persistence itself fails.
- A privacy rule keyed on one payload shape fails on the next shape; attribute values to their column wherever a result names it, and test on the bytes the provider receives.
- A reduction seen on the tasks that produced a fix can fail to replicate on fresh tasks; report it as local until it does.
- A reference library's missing-value policy can differ by role; check every role, not only the regressors.
- A replay is evidence only under the recorded project setup; diff the first divergence before reading anything after it.
- A requirement must be reachable from what the gate shows: the record a check reads is bound by the Workbench regardless of what else the Agent names, listed in the catalog, and named in a repair step whenever it is missing. A missing item with empty repair_steps is a gate defect.
- A requirement must come from the person or the data and be satisfiable; a refused part of an update must not discard the valid rest.
- A round guard must name every way a provider can refuse service and stop on the first one that makes further tasks meaningless.
- A selected group is presentation, not new authority: confirm each immutable revision/source, stop future confirmations and polling on cancellation/unmount, preserve actual receipts and resume once. Prove sibling chain semantics with real stores rather than an invalid two-head mock.
- A self-review of each fix found two new defects in the fixes themselves (a factory-name identity and a size-only index); review the fix against the failure class, not only the reported case.
- A server-issued reference binds source and plan but grants no permission. Resolve it into the ordinary admission path and preserve original call identity and logical retry scope. A cancelled task may never enter its coroutine, so the owner still closes its receipt; strict non-owner recovery remains unchanged. Scoped review and offline checks are not a release or real-model quality result.
- A terminal child event is a wakeup, not a completed operation receipt. Every UI/Auto/headless owner must use the same original provenance reconciler before subsequent result reads; keep the original approval, source, budget and idempotency key. Cancellation preserves takeover and missing original WAL remains explicit recovery.
- A timeout on a wrong task can hide a second cause of the wrong answer; judge a fix by accuracy on tasks that did not produce it.
- Admit and retain the directory before variable-duration preparation, then create, update and hash through directory-relative file descriptors.
- After validating the original content SHA and stable non-device identity, bind the current mount in a separate worker manifest and retain both manifest hashes.
- Agreement with a mirrored library formula is not an independent statistical oracle. Align full instrument spaces, test algebraic invariance, and retain correctly scoped scientific unavailability.
- An admitted proposal must be executable: resolve the source node when the proposal is made, and fail the operation record (not the server) when it stops resolving.
- An evaluation instrument is code under test: verify it on a pilot (request counts, hashes) before trusting a round.
- An expired timestamp is not proof that a local worker has stopped. Hold local execution exclusion through actual effect lifetime; preserve separate reconciliation rules after process death.
- An explicitly declared specification must not be completed from auto-detection; when any panel column is declared, an undeclared one means no effect.
- An independent oracle (R) catches definitional deviations that agreement with the reference library cannot.
- An invalid permission store is an explicit configuration error, never evidence that no restrictions exist.
- An optional argument whose absence triggers an expensive fallback needs a check at every call site; added lint rule ols-call-dataset-snapshot.
- Any durable-log reader on the per-step path must use the shared incremental index, never a whole-file parse.
- Any ledger replacement must carry the requests already used, since a cap counted per ledger is not a cap.
- Any offline experiment blocks the network at the HTTP layer first, so a missed patch fails instead of billing.
- Apply the shared deny decision before publishing a card. Guide only separately permissioned refits of an exact existing specification and bind post-estimation to the fitted run.
- Attribute a memory ratio stage by stage before changing code; the whole-run gap mixed a small new-step cost with retained state and first-run outliers.
- Before a long round, check the machine stays open; decide what happens to interrupted tasks before reading their grades.
- Before attributing peak-memory variance to code, split footprint into live malloc bytes, reusable and compressed pages; self-written output that Spotlight indexes can compress the next run's pages.
- Bind a new native binary to its source and ABI and retain the accepted default until the new binary has its own numerical, resource, cancellation and default-path acceptance; prototype timings are not transferable.
- Bind acceptance to the executing worker receipt and the retained input and host evidence, in addition to the disk build identity.
- Bind analysis to the selected data owner, validate saved model identity, and distinguish model application from new-data accuracy. Offline functional acceptance does not certify real Agent efficacy.
- Bind delivery scope to the task and target to producer evidence; import must not require unrelated estimation and optimizer success must be checked against feasible optimality.
- Bind focused recovery to the actual human request and verified confirmation continuation chain. Do not weaken completion or borrow results from another question. Browser receipt and persisted output are separate from real-model effectiveness.
- Bind task authority to real user provenance; separate scientific review basis from execution progress, and journal actual identities at their allocation owner. Scoped offline fixes do not establish live efficacy.
- Bound every server-written message the model reads, not only tool results; measure request anatomy per message from tapes after each smoke.
- Bound provider interruptions where they happen (per task) and end only that task; a round-level count turns one slow task into a missing round.
- Budget new default-path evidence and inference by elapsed time, peak memory and artifact bytes; retain alternating baseline/candidate raw data and never replace failed acceptance with profiler or correctness tests.
- Build and check must enumerate the same production variants. Missing identity is a failure, not evidence of a current build; a new verified manifest must not retroactively authenticate old unknown artifacts.
- Bytes and whitespace are not meaningful Agent progress. Explicitly bound generated output, preserve true final usage before handling length, and never turn a truncated tool request into execution or an automatic JSON retry. Offline containment does not certify generation quality; unknown-usage stops remain binding.
- Calculate derived report values from their registered machine evidence, never transcribe additional precision by hand.
- Cancellation of an async wait is not proof of native computation termination. Keep ownership through real completion and bookkeeping; immediate run cancellation and process-restart recovery remain open.
- Check free storage and candidate-bound approval before a formal run. Preserve the frozen roster and untouched held-out scope.
- Check human input persistence across delayed responses and actual constrained layout, not only mocked successful requests.
- Check provider protocol requirements against the request actually sent; a tolerant API is not evidence of a correct request.
- Check the actual frontend proxy with browser Origin and a no-op save before handing off credential setup; configure the preview origin explicitly instead of weakening the request guard.
- Check the actual provider hierarchy and durable external lifecycle, not only mocked callbacks or local submission state. Viewing recorded work must not resume or execute it.
- Check visible effect state against durable permissions and records. Exercise valid intake workflows with a registered family.
- Classify the visible command head and real child processes, rather than multiline shell literals.
- Compare wall and monotonic time and check the power log before attributing a long request to the provider.
- Composition must preserve each operation identity for deny, safety-floor and ask decisions before parent authorization; confirmation shares that decision.
- Compute spend figures from the tapes before recording them.
- Constrain min-content at grid/container/input boundaries, keep editable values out of React identity keys, and verify dense real catalogs plus expanded previews in a browser; model window caps must not exceed declared capacity.
- Content identity persists across environments; device/inode registration belongs to the current verified measurement lifetime. Never overwrite old input evidence or weaken within-run checks to bypass an environmental mismatch.
- Count model requests from the provider tape, not assistant messages; server-written messages look like replies.
- Cross-run column identity needs a verified dataset fingerprint or the owning run. Reproduce view-only lineage defects from immutable execution evidence before changing a producer.
- Decline the exact denied revision, preserve the permission evidence, and check revised proposals under the existing rules.
- Define a rate from the code and raw records before acting on it; separate protective refusals from defects so a lower number is not bought by weaker checks.
- Demonstrate a feature by a real turn in the interface the user is looking at; a seeded message may only be used for layout and must be disclosed.
- Derive model artifact contracts from actual validated workflow steps in one owner. A top-level label must not grant unrelated artifact requirements.
- Distinguish an observed analysis failure from provider overload and preserve every failed attempt. An SSE error inside HTTP200 must be recognized before local close cancellation masks its cause; never treat missing usage as zero or silently retry. Frozen graders lacking NVIDIA cache fields cannot certify zero cache hits.
- Do not extend a stopped sampling budget or infer whole-cohort quality from an unfinished fixed task.
- Do not mark a spike resolved until its holder is identified.
- Do not promote component speedup or a stable local phase into full-workflow acceptance. Keep every failed calibration and protocol deviation, attribute phases before adjusting the experiment, and retain the eight-second OLS gate.
- Do not widen a named503 exception into general unknown-usage recovery. Preserve partial work and distinguish raw tape accounting from frozen grader placeholders for interrupted tasks. General type/contract/reference feedback repairs require independent offline checks and cannot retroactively improve retained real results.
- Every evaluation script pins the model it means to measure and checks the first request's model before trusting the round.
- Every requirement the gate reports as missing needs an executable next step derived from server-known facts; ordering matters when a later product must cover an earlier one.
- Every value the server can determine must be bound by the server; a model should only make choices.
- Execution progress must come from persistent successful receipts, while original done labels are not authority. Changed dependencies need human reapproval; OS notification submission must not be called visible delivery.
- Exercise saved-version downloads through the browser and record the remaining scenario-specific gaps separately.
- Expose bounded existing producer risk summaries with source identity, not a new reliability verdict. Delivery completion, method sufficiency and estimate recovery are separate evidence.
- Fingerprint code must be tested on every column dtype the importer produces, including dates.
- For the exact Ultra route, request final usage accounting and read the bounded completion tail before exposing tools or done. Retain true interruption failures and original unknown usage; offline synthetic tail replay cannot reconcile an abandoned live request. Other NVIDIA models retain their prior protocol.
- Freeze the planned denominator, thresholds and exact recipient before any formal model or sealed evaluation. Keep offline browser and manual replication evidence separate.
- Function-body AST equivalence is insufficient when registry publication depends on module initialization order. Preserve the old import order during a move-only extraction.
- Gate tests for a playbook must use real fits from the engine, not hand-built payloads: the outcome_family unit fixture supplied a fingerprint no real logit or probit fit writes. The new tests run real Logit/Probit workflows, and the recorded open.r1 project now misses only its report.
- Guard shared services and delivery, retain the original project through pinned rendering, and recheck previously generated downloads.
- Keep both analysis-ready and requested-delivery clocks. An observed memory gap does not waive the predeclared noise gate, and incomplete native tracing cannot replace process RSS. Retain failed evidence and change the investigation rather than repeatedly rerun the same workload.
- Keep calibration failures and raw observations even when candidate medians look better; component measurements do not certify default-path end-to-end performance.
- Keep cold public-call, whole worker, evidence-verification allocations and native allocations separate. Retain failed A/B data and source binaries; a revised observer must re-register and preserve all numerical/resource/noise gates rather than transferring local speed into default-path acceptance.
- Keep exact cohort definitions and family/truth scoring separate from the execution and sealed exposure receipts.
- Keep gateway refusal and unknown billing distinct from statistical failure. Stopped free-route ledgers require explicit resolution, not storage exceptions or unapproved alternate-model attempts.
- Keep improvements that meet numerical and relative resource gates, while retaining the absolute end-to-end failure and profiling the remaining real owner work.
- Keep provider-specific reasoning controls behind their provider/model boundary; configure explicit supported efforts and verify the actual bounded tool request with mocks before live access.
- Keep task outcome, actual provider lifetime, interrupted usage and lease state separate. Do not expand recovery exceptions or call an attempted task successful; replay the concrete lifecycle before resuming.
- Keep the 8-second target unchanged. Distinguish exact fingerprint compatibility, isolated function timing, script budgets, end-to-end acceptance and measurement-tool failures. Profile the failing stage rather than rerunning already-passed unrelated budgets.
- Localize a claimed platform failure with immutable wire arguments, schema and real receipts. State caller type errors and non-execution explicitly without coercing malformed strings or taking model self-diagnosis as evidence. Offline feedback repair does not establish live improvement.
- Locate live allocation owners and distinguish delayed figure collection from native resident-page changes; never certify default-path I/O with a benchmark that substitutes writes.
- Make model-facing declarations deterministic at their owner; do not weaken full-field replay or contract comparison to hide ordering drift.
- Map each objective criterion to its own evidence; do not substitute a 5% regression budget or scripted replay for absolute latency and real-analysis acceptance.
- Match task concurrency to the serialized provider throughput and unchanged per-command wall. Preserve the timeout, failed tasks and unknown usage; do not bypass the stop or blame the provider without evidence. Prepare a serial-task candidate for an explicit continuation decision.
- Measure a prompt or recovery rule on the recorded short tasks as well as the long ones before changing it; the short tasks carried the cost of all three changes.
- Measure per-step server overhead on a real long conversation, not only on a small fixture.
- Measure the actual owner workload and requested allocations before promoting vectorized metadata code; reject regressions without replacing or excluding failed evidence.
- Measure the effective live declaration and adapter path before extending architecture; module counts and dynamic registration alone do not prove reduced capability implementation cost.
- Measure where agent time goes from the provider tape before choosing a speed target.
- Measure which declarations are ever used before paying for them in every request; a schema branch outside its own enum is pure cost.
- Native diagnostic binding must use the exact model companion. Inspection and operation receipts must expose the same actionable recovery without weakening evidence or approval gates.
- Numeric spelling is not statistical meaning; do not let year/prose exemptions bypass explicit sample counts.
- Opening actual history is insufficient UI proof: inspect message geometry with dense result navigation. Keep prose and secondary references on separate rows and locally contain wide Markdown.
- Oracle-test the path the consumer calls, not only the kernel; a kernel test with hand-built design matrices cannot see what the adapter leaves out.
- Persist small actual producer definitions with the original fingerprint and verify typed scientific products against the original execution, registered source and approval basis. Existing reports or prose do not replace AR evidence; old missing definitions remain partial. Ask can reuse an exact human-confirmed request while Auto retains approved-plan requirements.
- Pre-header protocol disconnection is not the approved ReadError class. Preserve the unknown reservation and stop; do not relabel errors or bypass the shared retry contract.
- Present signed evidence and actual count roles with registered input identity before judging a reported effect.
- Preserve bounded model context while making full local ledgers independently inspectable. Judge browser recovery against actual durable records; do not infer failure from lost tool feedback or pass from supervisor exit zero.
- Preserve failed calibration, separate mathematical equality from timing acceptance, and investigate phase noise before another distinct measurement.
- Preserve received headers, generated-content evidence and the local cancellation actor separately. A response header is not generation or usage; an empty interrupted HTTP200 cannot inherit a partial-text or pre-header retry grant.
- Preserve unknown usage and both local/transport failures; do not claim a rate-ceiling violation, silently raise recovery limits, or credit new product fixes to an old frozen cohort.
- Preview must reject deterministic native budget failures, and review must require the receipt that defines an estimated choice.
- Probe each gateway control value, including combinations, before relying on it.
- Probe the publication boundary with changed inputs, rather than stopping after checking independent hash and decode operations or representative syntax forms.
- Probe the recorded request counterfactually before changing prompts; one-step probes did not move the model, so the fix states product facts (the focus names a selection, not the analysis; a declared outcome.type_served caution names the families that serve the outcome) instead of forcing Logit.
- Prompt text did not move the model at the decision step (0 of 18 samples); a product-enforced check before the reply and a gate that reads the family declarations did. Weak instruction followers need enforced checks, not more rules.
- Publish native libraries with a fresh inode and atomic replacement. Verify bytes, source and ABI against the build receipt after publication.
- Publish receipts atomically before treating delivery as complete; cover exceptional terminal paths. A declared capability also needs manual discovery words and explicit reachability registration. Preserve original full-suite failures and rerun affected paths only.
- Read harness confirmation statuses when a task loops: http_500 was recorded and ignored for many rounds.
- Read the phase integration contract before adding any model-backed role. Keep requests and cumulative budgets in Core; pure review transitions can replay original events, while mutating phases remain reconciliation-only.
- Read the writer of a recorded field before computing from it, and measure a proposed saving on recorded data before quoting it.
- Reconcile persisted tool-to-operation identity and cumulative budgets before recovery; preserve legacy HTTP response contracts and domain authority boundaries.
- Refusal rates so far omit plan refusals reported under ok true with output.error; count both, and judge accuracy only on the next full round.
- Register and execute a fixed-environment measurement under one actual parent invocation. Preserve the complete inherited environment and original failed registration; do not drop varying fields or relax source, thread, noise or resource checks. A repaired preflight does not establish performance acceptance.
- Registries built at import depend on import order: new tools import heavy services when they run, and a subprocess test guards it.
- Reload local provider state without remote calls; diagnose timeout phase with a minimal no-tool control before changing tool code. Mark unanswered remote endpoints unverified and retain accounting uncertainty rather than looping or claiming success.
- Replay needs auditable business identity and time at their real owners, plus initial-state and effect verification. Do not remove semantic request fields, replay safety lease clocks, or fall back to a provider.
- Report source decomposition and extension cost separately; shortening entries does not reduce operation-registration seams.
- Require all seven core packets, require advanced evidence for predeclared workloads and whenever registered, and compare all published fields including presence. Keep distinct inference and statistical capture checks.
- Require planning before execution. Verify project denial, preparation-only semantics and actual workflow members before granting plan authority.
- Reserve one active benchmark tree, save all observations and complete logs, and publish representatives only after validation. Check APFS private extents and re-read bytes before reclaiming space.
- Reuse only structurally identical declarations proven present in the same model request; preserve durable ToolResults, source and approvals and retain changed schemas in full. Expose set topology, actual named diagnostics and proof scope explicitly. Exact numerical-field checks do not certify free-text science. Existing work needs binding, while justified missing work may use the original permission path. Live accuracy and latency remain unverified.
- Reuse the authority owner identity; model parameters and trusted server context are different trust boundaries.
- Review packets must retain needed producer aggregates or declare incomplete; unreadable evidence is an actionable refusal before human confirmation, never an empty success. Preserve editable-target early rejection and original failures; scoped fake-model evidence is not live accuracy.
- Run the full suite, and a replay of the short-question round, before judging an Agent change measured on five long positions; two writers in one worktree need an explicit split.
- Sample memory for the whole process tree during long runs; a peak of a process that exited cannot be recovered afterwards.
- Score a simulated task against what its fixed draw supports, and check every fixed seed's oracle before freezing a grader.
- Select automatic tool mode for NVIDIA Ultra and keep the one-tool surface and Workbench validation. Preserve the stopped request ledger.
- Send recurring import and direct-run-file faults to lint with the public replacement. Bind lookalike estimands to outcomes and score holdout correctness from verified rubric fields.
- Separate a model's effects from the data's structure; a fix to one must keep the other.
- Separate display identifiers from canonical registered references, reject invalid concrete evidence without blocking legitimate partial plans, and preserve actionable bounded errors and truthful qualified completion.
- Separate module retirement, manual runtime evidence and local UI appearance from real-provider evaluation and release gates.
- Separate once-per-model transport/tool smoke from complete Agent evaluation; retain no-response and unknown usage without inferring unsupported tools or zero cost, and do not turn another explicitly authorized model probe into an invisible fallback.
- Separate the transport endpoint from the final data-processing boundary. Unknown and historical configurations receive remote protection; invalid configurations must not select another provider.
- Size a paid round's cap as expected spend plus one worst-case request reservation.
- Stop immediately after journaling an observation whose host exclusivity cannot be verified, and monitor the campaign only through the runner's own output.
- Test model-facing required inputs and backward compatibility at the route, then separately verify full persisted binding and stale-head refusal.
- Trace each statistic to its event definition before imposing a cross-field identity. A number-bag check does not certify field semantics.
- Treat plan budgets as patches; distinguish omitted restrictions from explicit empty ones. Preserve the established zero-as-unlimited contract but require human reapproval for expansion. Test real Auto admission after updates, not only serialized plan values; no-op parameter validators or unrelated durations cannot certify L1 feasibility or execution estimates.
- Two consumers of one evidence set must share one definition: report sources now use the gate's computed_result predicate, and the regression test runs the gate's own compose_report through report.compose on real fits and a real exploration until the gate reports nothing missing.
- Use native-currency worst-case reservations; unknown usage stops the whole round. Return typed, source-verified artifact bindings and distinguish primary ambiguity from a missing model without weakening scientific completion.
- Use one provider waiting policy across transport and Agent, preserve explicit overrides and independent abort; distinguish documented free entitlement, model-card limits, hosted limits and observed runtime outcomes.
- Use original input and manifest-verified raw software output as the independent numeric oracle. Preserve a failed derived-summary digest instead of replacing its manifest or calling that summary verified.
- Use predeclared same-input blocks to measure millisecond component calls and retain the original observations. Separate numerical verification, time, and resource claims.
- Use the complete tracked evaluation artifact rather than a mutable one-page checklist as the primary failure evidence.
- Use the original owner clock at the actual send boundary; preserve in-flight failures and measure context bytes without claiming real model efficacy. A configured silence window is not a total generation guarantee.
- Validate model citations by what they point to, not by exact spelling, and keep refusing references outside the supplied evidence.
- Validate readiness at execution, not merely the DAG shape at plan creation.
- Verify agent display changes in a real turn on the composer actually mounted, with real result payloads.
- Verify each explicit promise using its fixed scientific definition and original producer artifacts. Preserve partial and unsupported states; reuse old results without inventing execution history, and keep scoped checks distinct from whole-release acceptance.
- Verify final serialized egress, isolate per-request outputs, bind complete execution specifications, and test shape-sensitive resource costs. Process verification does not certify release acceptance.
- Version a separate storage representation and verify exact restoration before using it at an immutable artifact boundary.
- When a failure has a sharp ceiling equal to one of our own bounds, check the harness path end to end (probe without it) before blaming the provider.
- When a round slows its own request starts, subtract the harness's waits from the task's limit and record them beside the raw wall time (user decision 2026-11-07); the limit, nudges and stop rules stay the same.
- When both sides change one function, review and retain a complete implementation from one side before applying follow-up changes. Exercise the function contract before a full suite.
